tā eṉṉum ōr aṭippaṭit tamiḻ viṉiccol, ilattīṉilum kirēkkattilum vētamoḻiyilum palvēṟu vaṭivil tirintu vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. tamiḻiṉ mutaṉmiyiyum mūlat taṉmiyiyum kāṭṭa iḵtoṉṟē pōtum. ataṉāl, iti maṟukkumāṟu, īrāṇṭiṟku muṉpē par.te. po. mī. avarkaṭku ōr aṟikūval viṭuttēṉ. ataṟku ituvari maṟumoḻi yilli.
kīḻiyāriyar kālnaṭi mēyttalic ciṟapput toḻilākak koṇ ṭiruntaṉareṉpatu, kōttiram eṉṉum kuṭumpap peyarālum tuhitru eṉṉum makaṭpeyarālum, uḻavuttoḻili mēṟkoḷḷavilli yeṉpatu, avar nāṭōṭit taṉmiyālum,  mēliyāriya moḻikaḷi luḷḷa ēr eṉṉun teṉcol avar moḻiyi liṉmiyālum, uḻavut toḻilik kolittoḻileṉṟu avar taruma cāttiram paḻippatālum; aṟiyappaṭum. (kō = āṉ (pacu), tuh = kaṟa) avar moḻikku mēṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya peyar maṟintoḻintatu. āriyam, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum iraṇṭum avar intiyāviṟku vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē tōṉṟiyavi. āriyam eṉṉum col intiyāviṉiṉṟē mēli nāṭukaṭkup paraviṟṟu.
āriyam eṉpatu tamiḻc collē. aru -āri = arumi, aḻaku, mēṉmi. āri yākavañ cāntan taṉittapiṉ (cīvaka. 129) āri - āriyaṉ = mēlōṉ. itu āriyarē tamiḻari  ēmāṟṟit tamakku iṭṭukkoṇṭatu.  ‘aristos’ (best) eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṟkum aru eṉpatē mūlamāyiruttal vēṇṭum. ēr-Ar-Arya eṉpatu tavaṟu.
kum - cum - cam(sam). kummutal = kuvital, kūṭutal, kalattal, karumi = mikuti, perumi. kaḷḷutal = kalattal, kūṭutal. kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭṭam, avi. kaḷ - kar - karu. karuttal = mikutal, mikuttal, ceytal. karu - karumam. karu - karuvi. karu - karaṇam. karu(va.) kru - kruta. samkruta - samskruta = pirākirutamum tamiḻum, allatu vētamoḻiyum tamiḻum kalantu ceyyappaṭṭa moḻi. prākruta camaṟkirutattiṟku muntu ceyyappaṭṭa moḻi. putitākac ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷāl ap poru ḷiṉam  mikutal kāṇka. iṉi, karuttal = ki karuttuk kāḻppēṟu maḷavu uḻittuc ceytal eṉṟumām. karuṅkik kollar (cilap. 5:29) eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. karuttal eṉṉum viṉi tamiḻil muṉpē vaḻakkaṟṟatu. pirā = muṉ. kruta = ceyyappaṭṭatu
tamiḻar ki.mu. 100,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumarināṭṭil  tōṉṟiya mūla makkaḷiṉattāriṉ vaḻivantavar. iṟṟi ilakkiyattiṉum virivāṉa, eḻunilac ceyyuḷāliyaṉṟa paltuṟi muḻut tūya ilakkiyattiyum muttamiḻilakkaṇattiyum koṇṭiruntavar.

oruporuḷ nutaliya cūttirat tāṉum

potumoḻi kiḷanta paṭalat tāṉum
mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiya piṇṭat tāṉumeṉ
ṟāṅkaṉi marapi ṉiyalu meṉpa.	(cey. 166)
nēriṉa maṇiyi niralpaṭa vittāṅ
kōriṉap poruḷi yoruvaḻi vippa
tōtteṉa moḻipa vuyarmoḻip pulavar. 	(cey. 168)
oruneṟi yiṉṟi viraviya poruḷāṟ
potumoḻi toṭariṉatu paṭala mākum. 		(cey. 169)
mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiya taṉmit tāyiṟ
ṟōṉṟumoḻip pulavar piṇṭa meṉpa 	(cey. 170)
eṉa viḷakki, īruṟuppaṭakkiya nūl eṉṟum, mūvuṟuppaṭakkiya piṇṭam eṉṟum, nūli iruvakiyāka vakuttuk kūṟiṟṟu.
mēṟkūṟiya piṇṭa nūṟpāviṟku.
am mūṉṟu uṟuppiṉiyum aṭakki varuvatu piṇṭam eṉṟavāṟu.
 am mūṉṟaṉiyum uṟuppeṉavē, piṇṭameṉpaṉatām uṟuppiṉaveṉpatu peṟṟām. tolkāppiyam eṉpatu piṇṭam;  ataṉuḷ eḻuttatikāram, collatikāram, poruḷatikāra meṉpaṉa paṭalameṉap paṭum; avaṟṟuḷ ōttuñ cūttiramum oḻinta iru kūṟumeṉappaṭum.
tōṉṟumoḻip pulavar piṇṭa meṉpa eṉṟataṉāl, piṇṭat tiṉiyum aṭakkiniṟpatu vēṟu piṇṭamuḷateṉpatu. atu mutaṉūlākiya akattiyamē pōlum; eṉṉi? aḵtu iyaṟṟamiḻ, icittamiḻ, nāṭakat tamiḻeṉṉum mūṉṟu piṇṭattiṉiyum aṭakki niṟṟaliṉ eṉṟu urittār pērāciriyar.
ottu eṉiṉum iyal eṉiṉum okkum. atikāram eṉiṉum paṭalam eṉiṉum pāl eṉiṉum okkum.
pāṇiṉīyam cūttiram, pātam, attiyāyam eṉṉum mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiyatēṉum atip piṇṭameṉṉum vaḻakku vaṭamoḻiyililli. mēlum, atu eḻuttum collum ākiya iraṇṭē kūṟuvatāl, īruṟup paṭakkiyatākavē koḷḷappaṭum. ātalāl, atu nūlaṉṟip piṇṭamākātu.
tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa poruḷatikāram eṉṉum mūṉṟā muṟuppu, yāppu, aṇiyeṉṉum potuvakik kūṟukaḷoṭu vēṟem moḻiyilu millāta poruḷilakkaṇa meṉṉum taṉicciṟappuk kūṟṟik koṇ ṭiruttalāl, tolkāppiyattuk kiṇiyāṉa ilakkaṇanūl iv vulakil oṉṟumē yilliyeṉpatu teḷḷat teḷivām. poruḷilakkaṇattip pāṭṭiyaleṉṟu kūṟi ataṉ perumiyik kuṟikkavum maṟikkavum muyalpavar tam aḻukkāṟṟiṉ aḷaviṉmiyik kāṭṭuvatōṭu, nēr niṉṟu karuṅkākkiyi veṇpuṟā veṉṉum kaṭuntuṇiccaṟ poyyarē yāvar.
iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷuri kūṟum toṉṉūlkaḷi nampātum, tolkāppiyar āṅkāṅkuk kuṟippiṭum muṉṉūlkaḷik karutātum, vaṭanūlkaḷilillāta tolkāppiyap poruḷilakkaṇattik kaṇṭuṅ kāṇātatupōṟ kaṇmūṭiyum,  naṉṉūlum ciṉṉūlumpōl eḻuttuñ collumē kūṟum pāṇiṉīyattiṉiṉṟu mūṉṟuṟuppaṭakkiya piṇṭamāṉa tolkāppiyan tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu, pūṉi vayiṟṟiṉiṉṟu pulikkuṭṭi  vantateṉṟum, narimā arimāvi īṉṟateṉṟum kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatē.
uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvakiyeḻuttuk koṇṭa neṭuṅkaṇakku, intiyāvil maṭṭumaṉṟi ulakilēyē mutaṉmutal tamiḻiltāṉ tōṉṟiṟṟu. atip piṉpaṟṟiyē pirāmiyum kirantamum tēvanākariyum tōṉṟiṉa. itu piṉṉoru kaṭṭuriyil virivāka viḷakkappaṭum.
vētamantiraṅkaṭkuc ciṭci eṉṉum oliyiyalum, pirāticākkiyam eṉṉum taṉimaṟi allatu mantirat tokutikkuriya ilakkaṇa muṟiyum, tamiḻilakkaṇattip piṉpaṟṟiyē eḻuntaṉa.
intiya āriyariṉ vaḻakkiṟanta tāymoḻiyum pirākirutamuṅ kalantatē vētamoḻi. vētamoḻiyum tamiḻum kalantatē camaṟkirutam eṉṉum palatuṟi ulakiyal ilakkiyamoḻi. vētamoḻiyic camaṟ kirutam eṉpatu muṟkālappaṭuttam (Prochronism) eṉṉum vaḻuvākum.
camaṟkirutattiṟku eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamē viyākaraṇam. mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiya aintiram eṉṉum camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam, tamiḻakatti lēyē tōṉṟit tamiḻakattilēyē aḻikkappaṭṭatu. aintirattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatē aṣṭātyāyī eṉṉum pāṇiṉīyam. tolkāppiyar pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭavarātaliṉ aintiram maṭṭum kaṟṟiruntār. ataṉā lēyē, aintiram niṟinta tolkāppi yaṉeṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟi eṉṟu paṉampāraṉār pāyirattiṟ pāṭiṉār.
tolkāppiyar kālam ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu; pāṇiṉi kālam ki.mu.5ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇam tamiḻilakkaṇattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟēyaṉṟi, tamiḻilakkaṇam  vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟavilli.
eḻuttumuṟi, eḻuttuccāriyi, eḻuttuppiṟappiyal, uyirmeyt taṉivaṭivam, puṇariyalvirivu, collamippu, colvaki, tokitoṭar vaki, eṇvēṟṟumi, mutaliyavaṟṟil, vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇam tamiḻilak kaṇattit taḻuviyiruppatu, teṟṟeṉat tōṉṟum. tolkāppiyar mūvatikārattiyum ovvoṉpatu iyalkaḷāka vakuttiruppatu pōṉṟu, pāṇiṉiyār eṇṇattiyāyattiyum nannāṉku pātamāka amit tiruppatu, kavaṉikkattakkatu. pāṇiṉīyam paṭippaṭiyākat tiruntivanta pala ilakkaṇa nūlkaṭkup piṟpaṭṭatākum. ataṉ muṉṉūlkaḷ 60 eṉpar kā.cu. piḷḷiyār.
patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutiyil, cārls vilkiṉcu (Charles Wilkins), uvilliyam cōṉcu (William Jones) mutaliya  mēliyaṟiñarāṟ camaṟkirutam veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭa piṉ, mēṉāṭṭār āriya oppiyaṉ moḻinūli vaḷarttaṉar. āyiṉ, āriyattiṉ mūlamoḻiyi aṟiyā miyāl, ammaṭṭil niṟuttikkoṇṭu ataṟku vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl (Descriptive Linguistics) eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. āriyam tirimoḻiyāta lāl, ataṉ vāyilāka vērccoṟkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ poruḷiyum mūlamoḻi tōṉṟiya vakiyiyum aṟiya muṭiyātu. avaṟṟi aṟivikkak kūṭiya iyaṉmoḻi tamiḻ oṉṟē. uṇmiyāṉa moḻinūl, oppiyalum vaṇṇaṉiyiyalum varalāṟṟiyalum oruṅkē koṇṭu koṭivaḻiyal (Geneological) muṟippaṭṭatākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. ātalāl, vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl eṉpatu ōr aṟiviya lākātu. itu piṉṉarum viḷakkappaṭum. 
camaṟkirutattik kaṇṭupiṭittapiṉ mēṉāṭṭār oppiyaṉ moḻinūli vaḷarttukkoṇṭatupōl, tamiḻarum pāṇiṉīyattip piṉpaṟṟiyē tamiḻilakkaṇam amittukkoṇṭaṉar eṉpatu, pēraṉē pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟāṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟatē. tolkāppiyar camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamuṅ kaṟṟiruntār eṉpatu uṇmiyē. āyiṉ. pāṇiṉīyattip piṉpaṟṟit tolkāppiyatti iyaṟṟiṉār eṉpatu aṟiyāmi paṟṟiyatē. iti avar kūṟṟum poruḷatikāramum teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭuttum. ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ tōṉṟiya talikkaḻakak kālattiliruntu eḻuttu col poruḷ eṉṉum mūvatikārap paṭṭu, yāppiyum aṇiyiyum poruḷuḷ aṭakki, oṇṭīntuṟi yeṉṟu māṇikkavācakarum viyakkumaḷavu nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakka akapporuḷ koṇṭu, marapāka vaḻaṅki vanta teyvat tamiḻilakkaṇatti, ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭil tōṉṟi eḻuttuñ collumē kūṟum pāṇiṉīyattiṉ vaḻiyatākak kūṟuvatu, tamiḻik kāṭṭik koṭukkum ceyaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭatēyaṉṟi vēṟākātu.
iṉi, vētak kālattil āriyar tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koḷḷa villiyeṉṟum, kāttiyāyaṉar kālamākiya ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭi lēyē at toṭarpu ēṟpaṭṭateṉṟum kūṟuvatum varalāṟṟoṭu muṟṟum muraṇpaṭṭatākum.
oru kālattil intiyā muḻutum tamiḻarē paraviyiruntaṉar. vaṭakkē cellac cella moḻiyum nāḷaṭivil mella mellat tirintatu. ataṉāl, vaṭanāṭukaḷip piṟkālattil moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟaṉar.
moḻipeyar paṉmali yiṟappiṉum
oḻital cellā toṇṭoṭi kuṇaṉē. 	(aiṅ. 321)
moḻipeyar tēen tarumār maṉṉar
kaḻippiṇik kaṟittōl nirikaṇ ṭaṉṉa. 	(akam. 67)
palvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum. 		(kuṟun. 11)
moḻimāṟu tēyam eṉṉātu moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟati nōkkuka. peyartal = orē moḻi mella mellat tirital.
kumarimaliyum paḵṟuḷiyāṟum mūḻkiyapiṉ, avaṟṟuk kīṭākap pāṇṭiyaṉ paṉimaliyiyum kaṅkiyāṟṟiyuṅ koṇṭāṉ. aṭiyārkkunallār ciṟappuk kuṟippip pārkka (cilap. 11:17-22).
kumarināṭṭuc civaneṟiyār aṇintu vantatum nēpāḷa nāṭṭuc civamaṇiyē (uruttirākkamē).
vaṅka nāṭu ceṉṟu vāḻnta tamiḻa vaṇikarē, kāḷikōyil camittuk kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉṟu nakarppeyariṭṭaṉar. aḵtu iṉṟu kalkattā eṉṟu āṅkilattil tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tamiḻakam (Tamluk) eṉṟoru nakarum an nāṭṭil tōṉṟiṟṟu. nilavāṇikamum nīr vāṇikamum vaṭanāṭukaḷoṭu toṭarntu nikaḻntuvantaṉa.
vaṭanāṭṭār kumariyāṭavum teṉṉāṭṭār kaṅkiyāṭavum vantu celvatu, toṉṟutoṭṭa vaḻakkamā yiruntuvantatu. pāratak kālattil arccuṉaṉum kumariyāṭa vantu pāṇṭiyaṉ kumariyi maṇantāṉ.
vēta āriyaruḷ mutaṉ mutalāka vintiyamali tāṇṭit teṉṉāṭu vanta āriya muṉivar akattiyar, ataṉāl vintamaliyiṉ ceruk kaṭakkiyavar eṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭār. avarukkup piṉ vantavar nāratar. piṉṉart takkaṇapatam eṉṉum oḻuṅkāṉa pātiyi amittuk koṇṭaṉar āriyar.
iyaṟkiyiyum aimpūtaṅkaḷiyum irucuṭariyum āvikaḷiyum cōmaccāṟāṉa kaḷḷiyumē vaṇaṅkivanta vēta āriyar, tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpukoṇṭa piṉṉarē, kāḷi vaṇakkattiyum kumara vaṇakkat         tiyum civatirumāl mataṅkaḷiyum mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉpiṉ patiṉeṇ purāṇaṅkaḷum itikācaṅkaḷum upaniṭataṅkaḷum ākamaṅ  kaḷum kāviya nāṭakaṅkaḷum viyākaraṇaṅkaḷum nikaṇṭukaḷum cāttiraṅ kaḷum piṟavum eḻuntaṉa.
iṅṅaṉamirukkavum, tolkāppiyamē mutal tamiḻnūleṉṟum, atuvum pāṇiṉīyattit taḻuviyateṉṟum mutaṉmutalāka naḷiyiru munnīr nāvā yōṭṭic (puṟam. 66) cuṟṟuk kaṭalōṭiyāyt (circumna vigator) tikaḻntu vaṭavi (Aurora Borealis) kaṇṭa tamiḻaṉik ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari vaṭintiyāvum aṟiyātavaṉ eṉṟu, iṟappa iḻittuk kūṟuvatu iḷañciṟārum eḷḷi nakiyāṭattakkatē.
kuṟippu: mūlat tamiḻ nāṭaka nūliṉiṉṟu moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭa kācumīra  vaṭamoḻip parata cāttiram, maṟiniḻalīṭāka mīṇṭum mūla moḻi yāṉa tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkappaṭa virukkiṉṟatu. itik kaṇṭu allatu kēṭṭu mūla moḻipaṟṟi mayaṅkaṟka.
nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlākiya paratam akattiyam mutalākavuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷum iṟantaṉa eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār tam urip pāyirattil, urittiruppatāl, vaṭamoḻi moḻipeyarppiṉ mūlat tamiḻp paratam aḻikkappaṭṭateṉa uyttuṇarka. itu  eṉ muttamiḻ eṉṉum nūlil virivāka viḷakkappaṭum.
kūṟṟu: acōkaṉum tamiḻnāṭum
cantirakuptaṉ kālattil, vitappāka acōkaṉ kālattē, (vaṭavar teṉṉavar) toṭarpu neruṅkiyiruntatu. acōkaṉ kaṭṭaḷi vācakaṅkaḷ piramakirivari teṟkiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ viṭuceytikaḷ tamiḻaracar uṭpaṭa aṉivarkkum viṭappaṭṭuḷa. avaroṭu toṭarpu neruṅkiyatāki yiruttal vēṇṭum. acōkaṉ cērar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, cattiyaputtirar allatu  atikamāṉar eṉṉum tamiḻnāṭṭaracar nālvarip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. avaruḷ mutal mūvarē aracareṉṟum, atikamāṉar  cēraroṭu uṟavukoḷḷa muyaṉṟa ciṟṟaracarē yeṉṟum, tamiḻ marapuvaḻic ceyti kūṟukiṉṟatu. caṅkak kālattilum tolkāppiyattilum in nili miyē kāṇkiṉṟōm. atikamāṉar vallamiyuḷḷavarāyiruntu ēṉi mūvaracaroṭum araciyal talimikkup pōṭṭiyiṭṭa kālam oṉṟu, ataṟku muṉ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. atu acōkaṉ kaṭṭaḷi vācakaṅkaḷ uruvāṉa kālamāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟi aṉittintiya eḻuttumuṟiyiṉ taḻuviya amippē yeṉṟum, atit tolkāppiyar kuṟippiṭukiṟār eṉṟum, iṉṉoru kaṭṭuriyiṟ collāṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. acōkaṉ viṭuceyti potumakaṉ uḷḷattik kavarntataṉāl, avaṉukku eḻuttaṟiviṉmītu vēṭki vaḷarntatu. atu aṉittintiya veḻuttit taḻuvit tamiḻeḻutti yamittaṟku akat tūṇṭukōlāyiṟṟu allatu ati virivupaṭuttiṟṟu. itu aṅṅaṉa māyiṉ, tolkāppiyam acōkaṉ kālattiṟkup piṉṉarē tōṉṟi yiruttal kūṭum.
puḷḷikkoḷki
puḷḷikkoḷki paṟṟiyum palaviṭattuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum ataṟkup piṉpumtāṉ puḷḷi tōṉṟukiṉṟatu eṉṟu  kuṟippiṭappaṭṭatu. veṭṭeḻuttukaḷiṉ eḻuttu muṟimi ilakkaṇak koḷkiyip piṉpaṟṟuvataṟku oru nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kālam ceṉṟirukku meṉṟu nām karutikkoḷḷalām. veṭṭeḻuttukaḷ potumakkaḷ ellārkkumeṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭavi. potu makkaḷ vaḻakkaṅkaḷ eḷitil māṟuvatilli. ātalāl, tolkāppiyam kiṟittavavūḻit tiruppattil allatu ataṟkuc caṟṟu muntit tōṉṟiya tākak koḷḷalām. eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, camaṟkirutattiṟ pōl eḻuttu muṟimikkut tēviyiṉṟiyē ilakkaṇak kūṟupaṭuppu vaḷarcci yaṭintirukkak kūṭiyatē. iṉi,  eḻuttu muṟiyip paṟṟiya kuṟippuka ḷellām piṟkāla vaḷarccikaḷ eṉpatum koḷḷakkūṭiyatē.
ilakkaṇam
āṉāl, aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum atu kāttiyāyaṉār kālattukku muntiyatākātu. kāttiyāyaṉarē ilakkaṇam eṉṉum collāṭciyi, `lakṣya' allatu vaḻakku eṉpati aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa aṇaṅ kattik (ilakkaṇattik) kuṟittateṉṟu viḷakka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ēṉeṉiṉ, tamiḻilakkaṇam tamiḻnāṭṭil ik kalviyiṉ neṭun toṉmi yik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyattil vevvēṟu paṭikaḷ
muṟkālattavar oruvarum piṟkālattavar oruvarumāka iruperu nūlāciriyar tolkāppiyar eṉṉum potuppeyar koṇṭiruntataṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa mayakkattiyum kuḻappattiyum yām ēṟkeṉavē viḷakki yirukkiṉṟēm. ik karuttuppaṭi mayakkam nērak kūṭiyatātal iṟṟit tolkāppiyattik kavaṉamākak kūṟupaṭutti āyat tūṇṭukiṉṟatu. attakik kūṟupāṭṭāyvutāṉ an nūliṉ palvēṟu paṭikaḷi veḷippaṭuttik kāṭṭavoṇṇum. ap paṭikku vēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷ paṟṟik kālaṅ kuṟittal vēṇṭum.
maṟuppu
acōkaṉ kālam ki.mu. 272-31. kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu mutaṟkāla makkaḷ vaṭakiḻakkum vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkumāka vaṭāciyāviṟkum airōppā viṟkum ceṉṟapiṉ, avar vaḻiyiṉar intiyāvukkut tirumpi varu muṉṉum, vaṭintiyāvari tiraviṭar perumpāṉmiyarākavum tamiḻar ciṟupāṉmiyarākavum paraviyiruntaṉar. tamiḻarē tiraviṭarākavum tiraviṭarē pirākirutarākavum māṟiyataṉāl, āriyar vanta kālattil vaṭintiyar perumpālum pirākirutarākavum, naṭuvintiyar perumpālum tiraviṭa rākavum iruntaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷalām.
ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari, camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattiṟ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum muttamiḻt teṉṉāṭṭaracukaḷē kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, ki.mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭa acōkaṉ kalveṭṭu kaḷil, pāṇṭiyam cōḻam kēraḷaputtiram (cēralam) eṉṉum muttamiḻ aracak kuṭikaḷuṭaṉ cattiyaputtirar eṉṉum vēṟōr araca marapiṉarum kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ac cattiyaputtirarit takaṭūrit talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭuvanta atikamāṉar eṉṉum ciṟṟaracak kuṭiyiṉarākap par. te. po. mī. kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
atikamāṉar oru paḻañcērar kiḷiyic cērnta ciṟṟaraca rātaliṉ, avarit taṉiyē pirittuk kūṟat tēviyilli. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil mūvēntaruk kaṭuttapaṭiyāka vallami yōṭiruntu, kōcar eṉṉum oru vakuppār tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭamēlip pakutiyāṉa tuḷunāṭṭi āṇṭu vantatāka, eṭṭuttokiyum pattuppāṭṭum cilappatikāramum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.

(cilap. uripeṟu kaṭṭuri.)

eṉṉum tolkāppiya aṭiyāl viḷaṅkum.
acōkaṉ kālattil atikamāṉ ki tamiḻakattil ōṅkiyirunta teṉpataṟku yātoru cāṉṟumilli.
iṟṟit tamiḻp pulaviyattuḷ tolkāppiyamē mutaṟ pottakamā yiruttal paṟṟi, ārāycciyillārellārum atuvē mutal tamiḻnūl eṉa mayaṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. viyāpuriyārō veṉiṉ, atiyum pāṇiṉīyattiṉ vaḻinūlākak kāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.
tolkāppiyam paṭukuḻikaḷum kavarneṟikaḷum niṟintatu. ātalāl, māntaṉ varalāṟṟārāycciyum moḻivaralāṟṟārāycciyum tamiḻ vērccollārāycciyum illātavar, ki.mu.1200 pōl tōṉṟiya  ilakkiyat tirimoḻiyākiya camaṟkiruta aṟivikkoṇṭu, ki.mu. nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya māntaṉ mutaṉbmāḻiyākiyatamiḻiyalpiaṟiyappukuvatu,naḻikoṇṭuāḻiyiaḷantaṟiyattuṇivatēbyākkum.acōkaṉ kalveṭṭuc ceytikaḷāṟ kavarappaṭṭa tamiḻakap potu makkaḷ, avaṟṟit tāmē paṭittaṟiya vēṇṭumeṉṉum avāviṉāl untappaṭṭu, avaṟṟiṉ pirāmiyeḻuttip payiṉṟukoṇṭaṉa reṉṟum. atuvē tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkut tōṟṟameṉṟum, par. te.po.mī. tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattāṟ kaṇṭiruppatu, ākā! ettuṇi arumiyāṉa uyttuṇarvu! itu viṇṇulakattum maṇṇulakattum vēṟevarkkum tōṉṟiyirukkak kūṭāta viḻuppula maṇikkaruttē! oru kallaṟit tōṭṭa vāyiliṉ mēl nāykaḷ pukaṟka! eṉṟu āṅkilattil eḻuti yirunta palakiyik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, oru nāy pukātu niṉṟuviṭṭa teṉṟu kūṟum pikkuvikkut tāḷkaḷi (Pickwick papers) viṭa itu nakic cuvi viñciyatē! itaṉāl, tikkeṉci (Dickens)  veṉṟuviṭṭār par. te. po. mī. yār eṉṟē collalām.
acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmiyeḻuttit tamiḻp potumakkaḷ kaṟṟapiṉ, tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟu, tamiḻi iḻittum paḻittum puṟakkaṇikka vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ karuttiruntālaṉṟi, tolkāppiyaṅ kaṟṟa evaruñ collat tuṇiyār.
meyyiṉ iyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal	(eḻut. 15)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvukkup piṉ,
ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē	(eḻut 16)
eṉṟuḷḷa nūṟpā tolkāppiyap paṭukuḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. itu  piṉṉar vēṟoru kaṭṭuriyil virivāka viḷakkappaṭum.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻutti ṉiṉṟu potumakkaḷ tōṟṟuvittateṉṉum muḻut tavaṟāṉa  karutti aṭippaṭiyāka vittē, par. te.po.mī. mēṟkoṇṭu ārāyntu cel kiṉṟār. mutaṟ kōṇal muṟṟum kōṇal eṉṟavāṟu, avar ārāycci aṭippaṭit tavaṟṟiṉāl muṟṟun tavaṟāka muṭikiṉṟatu.
uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvaki yeḻuttu vēṟupāṭum, avaṟṟukku varivaṭiva vēṟupāṭum uḷḷa neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟi ulakil mutaṉmutal tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu,  muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. meykkut taṉivaṭivillātu tōṉṟiya kukik kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭataṉāl, tamiḻ meyyeḻuttiṟkum puḷḷikkuṟi ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu, uṇmikku muṟṟum  māṟākum.
oru nāṭṭu makkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭutta an nāṭṭu aracaṉukkē iyalumātalāl, acōkaṉ taṉ āṭciyellikkuḷ poṟitta pāṟi, kuki, kampam ākiya mūviṭak kalveṭṭukaḷilum, taṉ  atikārattuk kuṭpaṭṭavarkkuk kaṭṭaḷiyākavum uṭpaṭāta ellippuṟa nāṭṭaracarkku naṭpu muṟippaṭṭa nalluriyākavum, tuṉpuṟuttāmiyi aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭa aṉpu neṟiyīṭukaḷik kuṟittatu, tamiḻakattip poṟuttavariyil aracari nōkkiyatēyaṉṟip potumakkaḷi nōkkiyataṉṟu. ōr aracaṉ naṭpu  niliyil maṟṟōr aracaṉiṉ kuṭikaṭku nēraṭiyāka ētēṉum eḻutuvatu iyalpumaṉṟu; muṟiyumaṉṟu.
kīḻvarum acōkaṉ kalveṭṭuc ceytiyi nōkkuka.
2ām pāṟik kalveṭṭu
(uvilcaṉ (Wilson) moḻipeyarppu)
“In all the subjugated territories of the king Priyadasi, the beloved of the gods, and also in the hordering countires, as Choda, Palaya (or Paraya) Satyaputra, Keralaputra, Tambapani it is proclimed, and Antiochus by name the Yona (or Yavana) raja and those princes who are nearer to or allied with that monarco, universally are apprised that two designs have been cherished by Priyadasi, one (design) regarding men, and one relating to animals; and whatever herbs are useful to men or useful to animals wherever there are none, such have been everywhere cused to be conveyed and planted and roots and fruits wherever there are none, such have been everywhere conveyed and planted; and on the roads wells have been cused to be dug, and trees have been planted for the respective enjoyment of animals and men.”
(Corpus Inscriptionum Indicarum, Vol I. pp. 117-8)
itil `Palaya' eṉṟatu pāṇṭiyaṉi; Tambapani (tāmpiraparaṇi) eṉṟatu ilaṅkiyi. acōkaṉ kalveṭṭuc ceyti cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya cattiyaputtirarākiya aracariyē  nōkkiyateṉpatu. itaṉāl teḷḷat teḷivām. mēlum kalveṭṭup poṟikkappaṭṭatu vaṭavellip puṟattilēyē yaṉṟit tamiḻakattuḷḷaṉṟu. ataṉāl tamiḻp potumakkaḷ ataṉi aṟintēyirār; kaṇṭirār eṉpatic collavē vēṇṭuva tiṉṟu.
oru moḻiyiṟ kuṟuṅkaṇakkākavēṉum neṭuṅkaṇakkākavēṉum vaṇṇamāli ēṟpaṭuvatu, aṟiñarālēyēyaṉṟik kallāp potu makkaḷālaṉṟu. ākavē, tamiḻap potumakkaḷ acōkaṉ kalveṭ ṭiṉiṉṟu tamiḻeḻuttik kaṟṟukkoṇṭatu, muṭavaṉ muṇmaramēṟik komputtēṉik koṇarnta ceytiyē yākum.
iṉi, eḻuttiṉṟiyē camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōṉṟu tamiḻilum tōṉṟiyirukkalā meṉṟum, tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa ilakkaṇak kuṟippukaḷellām, piṟkālattaṉaveṉṟum, par. te.po.mī. kūṟiyiruppatu, avarukkut tamiḻilakkaṇattip paṟṟit teḷivāṉa karuttiṉmiyit teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum col varalāṟu piṉṉart teri vikkappaṭum. 
kūṟṟu : tolkāppiyam eḻuttatikāram
I. nūṉmarapu
oliyaṉkaḷiṉ paṭṭi, avaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭu
tolkāppiyattiṉ mutaṟpākam eḻuttatikāram eṉappaṭum. ataṉ mutaṟpirivu nūṉmarapu, atāvatu tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiṉ marapu eṉappaṭum. tolkāppiyar mutaṟkaṇ eḻuttatikārattiyē iyaṟṟiṉā reṉṟum, ataṉāl  nūl eṉpatu eḻuttatikārattiyē kuṟikkumeṉṟum, oru karuttit terivittirukkiṉṟēṉ. nūṉmarapu tamiḻ olikaḷiyum  avaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭṭiyum meymmayakkaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kūṟu kiṉṟatu. avi yāvum eḻuttiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. kaṇṭuḷḷa viḷakkaṅ kaḷum iyalvariyaṟikaḷum perumpālum akkālattu vaḻakkilirunta neṭuṅkaṇakkolikaḷiṉ varici muṟiyit taḻuvi yuḷḷaṉa. akarattil toṭaṅki ṉakarattil muṭiyum mutaleḻuttukaḷ muppatu; avi iyaṟki yāka neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. mīṇṭum avar (neṭuṅ kaṇakkil) auvoṭu muṭiyum paṉṉīrolikaḷi uyir eṉṟum, avaṟ ṟukkup piṉ vantu ṉavvoṭu muṭiyum patiṉeṭṭi mey eṉṟum kūṟu kiṉṟār. ataṉpiṉ vevvēṟolikaḷiṉ aḷapu toṭarkiṉṟatu. av vaṭippaṭiyil, oru māttiriyuḷḷa uyir kuṟil eṉṟum, irumāttiri yuḷḷa uyir neṭil eṉṟum pākupāṭu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu. mey yeḻuttiṉ māttiri ari.
maṟuppu
vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttukaḷ mottam 48 eṉṟum, 52 eṉṟum, 53 eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum ilakkaṇa nūlāciriyariṭik karuttuvēṟupāṭiruntu varuvatu pōṉṟu, tamiḻilum cārpeḻuttuppaṟṟik karuttuvēṟupāṭu toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu vantirukkiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyar cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟeṉṟār. atu muṉṉūlkaḷit taḻuviyatu. pavaṇantiyār cārpeḻuttup patteṉṟār. iv viruvarkkum iṭippaṭṭa amitacākaraṉār, cārpiṟ ṟōṉṟun taṉmiya veṉṟā eṉṟu toki kuṟiyātu kūṟiṉum, ataṉ uriyāciriyar,

iṉi, tolkāppiyar kālattil nūl eṉṟatu potuvakippaṭṭa pottakattiyaṉṟu; ētēṉum oruvaki aṟiviyaliyē  (Science). iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻum taṉittaṉi ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ilakkaṇamellām aṟiviyal pōṉṟiruppatāl nūl eṉappaṭum. maruttuvam, kaṇitam, kaṇiyam (vāṉanūl) eṉpaṉavum avi pōṉṟa piṟavum aṟiviyalkaḷē yātalāl, aviyum nūleṉappaṭum.

eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviyum ataṉ uriyiyum nōkkuka.
ilakkaṇam oru moḻipaṟṟiya aṟiviyalā yiruttalāl nūl eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉik kūṟum pottakamum nūl eṉavēpaṭum. e-ṭu: mativāṇaṉār nāṭakat tamiḻnūl.
ākavē, nūṉmarapu eṉpatu, tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil marapākak koḷḷappaṭṭuvarum eḻuttukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭum eṉṟu poruḷ paṭumē yaṉṟi, nūṉmarapu oṉṟē tolkāppiyar mutaṉmutal eḻutiya ilakkaṇanūl eṉṟu poruḷkoḷḷa iṭantarātu.
eḻuttatikāram nūṉmarapu, moḻimarapu, piṟappiyal, puṇariyal, tokimarapu, urupiyal, uyirmayaṅkiyal, puḷḷimayaṅkiyal, kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal eṉat toṇ (oṉpāṉ) pakutippaṭṭiruppatum, nūṉmarapu eṉpatu eḻuttatikāram muḻuvatiyum kuṟikkaviyalāteṉpatit teṟṟeṉat terivikkum.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻutti ṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyateṉṟum, atiyē mutal tolkāppiyar nūlāka vaṭittār eṉṟum, ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām avarālum iraṇṭām tolkāppiyarālum pintic ceyyappaṭṭaviyeṉṟum, iru tavaṟāṉa aṭippaṭiyilēyē par. te.po.mī.yiṉ ārāycci iyaṅku vatāl, mutaṟkōṇal muṟṟuṅ kōṇalā yiruppati neṭukaluṅ kāṇalām.

kūṟṟu
puḷḷi avar (tolkāppiyar) cila eḻuttukaḷiṉ varivaṭivik kūṟap pukumiṭattup puḷḷikkoḷkiyip pukuttukiṉṟār. vēṟō riṭattil viḷakkiyuḷḷavāṟu, avar puḷḷiyuṭiya meyyeḻuttukaḷoṭu toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu viḷaṅkakkūṭiyatu. ekara okara uyirk kuṟilkaḷum puḷḷi peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. itu uyirmey eṉṉum aci yeḻuttippaṟṟik kūṟa vaḻikāṭṭukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiṟkiṭiyē mikak kuṟukiya makaramākiya makarakkuṟukkattiṉ vaṇṇaṉi varukiṉṟatu. ataṉ varivaṭivam puḷḷikku allatu meyyeḻuttiṟku muṉpē kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟatu. iṉṉō roḻuṅkiṉmiyum uḷḷatu. nūṉmarapu eḻuttoli kaḷit taṉitta vaṭiviṟ kūṟukiṉṟatu. moḻimarapu avaṟṟiṉ puṇariyal vēṟupāṭukaḷik kūṟukiṉṟatu. iv vēṟupāṭṭiṟku māṟāka, oru puṇariyal vēṟupāṭākiya makarak kuṟukkam nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. teḷivāka, itu ōr iṭiccerukal. mayilinātar makarakkuṟukkattiṟku mēṟkōḷ vēṟu nūlkaḷiṉiṉṟu kāṭṭit tolkāppiyattiṉiṉṟu kāṭṭāmi, ciṟappākak kavaṉikkat takkatu. makarakkuṟukkam paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpā piṟkālat tolkāppiyattic cērntatāyirukkalāmā?
maṟuppu
puḷḷiyeṉpatu tolkāppiyar putitāka vakuttataṉṟu. uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvaki yeḻuttum mutaṉmutalākak koṇṭa tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku, varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāta kumarināṭṭu viṉiyiṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu. meyyeḻuttiṟkuk kuṟiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu puḷḷi. itu piṉṉar virivāka viḷakkappaṭum.
tolkāppiyar nūṉmarapil eḻuttukaṭku aḷavu kūṟivarumiṭattu,
meyyi ṉaḷapē yariyeṉa moḻipa 	(11)
eṉṟu meyyeḻuttiṟku aḷapu kuṟittapiṉ, cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟum avvaḷapē koḷvatāl,
avviyal niliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē 	(12)
eṉṟu nūṟpāviyaṟṟi, ariyiṉuṅ kuṟukik kālāka volikkum makarak kuṟukkatti,
``ariyaḷavu kuṟukal makara muṭittē
iciyiṭaṉ arukun teriyuṅ kāli	(13)
eṉṟu kūṟi, ataṉ ciṟappāṉa varivaṭivatti,
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē	(14)
eṉṟu aṟivittār.
iṅṅaṉam, uriya iṭattilum toṭarcciyāṉa muṟiyilum kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷa puḷḷic ceytiyi,
meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal	(15)
eṉṉum puḷḷiyeḻuttup paṟṟiya nūṟpāvukku muṟpaṭak kūṟiyatu, iṭavaḻu eṉṉum kuṟṟameṉak koḷvatu poruntātu.

muppaḵteṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē.	(1)
avitām
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āytam eṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa		(2)
eṉṟu nūl toṭakkattilēyē puḷḷic ceyti kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl, ati āciriyaṉvāyk kēṭṭaṟinta māṇavaṉukku, uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē. eṉpatu putiyatākavum viḷaṅkātatākavum, muṟpaṭak kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatākavum, koḷḷappaṭā teṉka.
iṉi, tokuttuc cuṭṭal, vakuttuk kāṭṭal, virittuk kūṟal, oppiṉ muṭittal, oṉṟiṉam muṭittal, taṉṉiṉa muṭittal, moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉiyum muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal mutaliya uttikaḷi nōkkiṉum, uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē eṉṉum nūṟpā nūṉmarapil iṭam peṟṟiruppatu vaḻuvākat tōṉṟātu.
mayilinātar, naṉṉūliṉ cārpeḻuttut toki nūṟpāvukku,
eḻutteṉap paṭupa........mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
	eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum ivvāṟu mutal cārpeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭā reṉka. aḵtē, avar cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟē koṇṭā rālō veṉiṉ, aḵtē, naṉṟu coṉṉāy! oḻintavi eppāṟpaṭu meṉṟārkku mūṉṟāvatoru pakuti collalāva tiṉmi yāṉum, mutaleḻuttān taṉmi avaṟṟiṟkiṉmiyāṉum, cārpiṟ ṟōṉṟuta lāṉum, ip pattum cārpākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉpatu. aḵtē amika. oṟṟaḷapeṭiyum aikārak kuṟukkamum yāṇṭup aukārak kuṟukkamum makarak kuṟukkamum āytak kuṟukkamum peṟṟāmō veṉiṉ,
vaṉmiyoṭu raḵkāṉ ḻaḵkā ṉoḻintāṅ
kaṉmey āytamō ṭaḷapeḻum orōvaḻi.
aḷapeṭi taṉiyiraṇ ṭalvaḻi aiau
uḷatām oṉṟari taṉimiyu mākum.
patiṉeṇ meyyum atuvē mavvō
ṭāytamum aḷapari tēytalum urittē
eṉṟār āciriyar avinayaṉārum eṉak koḷka eṉa vurittuḷḷār.
itil, avinaya nūṟpāvē pōtumātaliṉ, tolkāppiya  nūṟpā eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭavilli. ataṉāl, tolkāppiya makarak kuṟukka nūṟpāvippaṟṟi nām aiyuṟa vēṇṭiyatilli. ikarak kuṟukkam, ukarak kuṟukkam, aikārak kuṟukkam, aukārak kuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam mutaliyaṉa vum tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum, avaṟṟiyellām mayilinātar mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭavilli, aṅṅaṉamē makarak kuṟukkamum eṉka.
iṉit tolkāppiya makarak kuṟukka nūṟpā piṟkālattatāyiṉ,
ceyyuḷ iṟutip pōli moḻivayiṉ
ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum. 		(moḻi. 18)
ṉakāri muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum 		(moḻi. 19)
eṉpaṉavum piṟkālattaṉavēyātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam ākāmi kāṇka.
kūṟṟu
oliyaṉkaḷiṉ pātīṭu
pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷum oliyaṉkaḷiṉ pātīṭṭi virittuk kūṟu kiṉṟaṉa. naṉṉūl ati mutalīṟiṭinili yeṉkiṉṟatu. tol kāppiyar, eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, mutalilumiṟutiyilumvarumoliyaṉkaḷimoḻimarapilum,meymmayakkaṅkaḷinūṉmarapilum,eṭuttukkūṟukiṉṟār.....im meymmayakkam  taṉic collilum toṭarc collilum kūṭṭuc collilum nikaḻum. eṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar taṉic collil vaḻaṅkum meymmayakkaṅkaḷiyē eṭuttuk kūṟi, eṭuttuk kāṭṭillāta meymmayakkaṅkaḷellām iṟantupaṭṭaṉa eṉṟoru koḷkiyi mēṟkoḷkiṉṟār. uṇmiyil nūṉmarapiṟ kovvāta cuṭṭuviṉā aṭikaḷum atiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
maṟuppu
āriya iṉam tōṉṟu muṉpē muttamiḻilakkaṇam kumarināṭṭil muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntataṉālum, mulli nākarika niliyiṉarāṉa āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta kālattil vaṭintiyāvil tamiḻarum vatintiruntataṉālum, irāmāyaṇak kālattilēyē akattiyar teṉṉāṭu vantu eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻ iyampi māpiṇṭamākiya akattiyam iyaṟṟiyataṉālum, āriya mutalilakkiyamākiya irukku vētam intiyā vilēyē iyaṟṟappaṭṭataṉālum, pala pirāticākkiya nūṟpākkaḷ tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷi ottiruppataṉālum, pāṇiṉīyattukku muntiya mutaṟ camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇamākiya aintiram tamiḻakattilēyē tōṉṟi maṟintataṉālum, pirāticākkiyaṅkaṭ kellām tamiḻilakkaṇamē mūlam eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi maliyām.
uṭaṉili meymmayakkam, vēṟṟunili meymmayakkam eṉṉum iruvaki meymmayakkaṅkaḷuḷ, piṉṉatip paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷiṉpaṭi, pala meymmayakkaṅkaḷ tokiccol eṉṉum kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷilum toṭarc coṟkaḷilumē nikaḻum.

	ṉva - puṉvaraku,	mva - varum vaṇṇakkaṉ,
	yca - eycili,	rca - vārcili,
	ḻca - vāḻcēri,	yña - pāyñekiḻi,
	rña - nērñekiḻi,	ḻña - vāḻñeṇṭu,
	rya - pōryāṉi,	ḻya - vīḻyāṉi,
	yṅa - vēyṅṅaṉam,	rṅa - vērṅṅaṉam,	
	ḻṅa - vēḻṅṅaṉam.
ñya, nya, mya, vya, eṉpavaṟṟiṟku uriyāciriyar kāṭṭiya uriñyātu, porunyātu, tirumyātu, tevyātu eṉpavaṟṟi  uriñ yāṉi, porunyāṟu, tirumyāmi, tevyāṭu eṉa māṟṟiṉ, nacciṉark kiṉiyar kūṟum kuṟṟam nīṅkiviṭum.
ellāt tokiyum orucol naṭiya	(ecca. 24)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamē kūṟuvatāl, meymmayakkattukkut taṉiccoṟ pōṉṟē kūṭṭuc collum  koḷḷappaṭum.
iṉṟu taṉiccol eṭuttukkāṭṭillā meymmayakkaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟukku, muṉkālattil taṉiccolliruntu piṉṉar iṟantupaṭ ṭirukkalām. āyiṉ, taṉiccollē eṭuttukkāṭṭukkut takum eṉa variyaṟuppatu poruntātu.
orē col allatu eḻuttamiti vevvēṟu vakiyil vevvēṟu iyalukku uriyatākalām. amma eṉṉum viḷippeyar kēṭpikkum iṭiccollāyiṉ iṭiyiyalukkuriyatu. karuvi eṉṉum tokutip peyar ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuppaṟṟi uriyiyalukkuriyatu. iṅṅaṉamē cila meym mayakkaṅkaḷum eḻuttiyalukkum puṇariyalukkum uriyaṉavākum. ātalāl, kaṭciṟār kaṟciṟār eṉpaṉa irumoḻip puṇarcciyākaliṉ īṇṭikkākā eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vilakkuvatu poruntātu.
iṉi, nya, ṇya, mya, ṉya eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa meymmayakkaṅkaḷ tamiḻil taṉiccoṟkup poruntāvātaliṉ, eṉṟum kūṭṭuc colli lēyē nikaḻum. avaṟṟit taṉiccoṟkum uriyaṉaveṉṟu kūṟi, tamiḻi vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamākkavum vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyateṉak kāṭṭavum muyalvar, akappakiyum puṟappakiyumākiya tamiḻppakivar cilar.
cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷum viṉāveḻuttukaḷum tamiḻ marapeḻuttu kaḷātaliṉ, aviyum tolkāppiya nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkut tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟu mayaṅkiyō mayaṅkātō kūṟuvataṉālum, ic cuṭṭeḻuttum viṉāveḻuttum vaṭamoḻiyiliṉmiyāla vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇattil kūṟappaṭāmiyālum, iv viruvaki yeḻuttukaḷum nūṉ marapiṟkup poruntāveṉap par. te. po. mī. kūṟuvatu avar piṟaḻ vuṇarcciyiyē piṟaṅkak kāṭṭum. 
kūṟṟu
moḻimarapu
aṭutta iyal moḻimarapu. itil avar i,u, āytam  eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉ kuṟukkaṅkaḷākiya puṇariyal vēṟupāṭukaḷik kūṟukiṟār. am mūṉṟum taṉiccoṟkaḷilum puṇarccoṟkaḷilum nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. camaṟkirutap pulutam (pluta) eṉṉum uyiraḷapeṭiyi aṟintukoṇṭu, irumāttirikku mēlolikkum neṭṭuyir tamiḻil illi yeṉṟu ēṟkeṉavē vācakari  eccarittirukkiṉṟār. āyiṉ, āṟām nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiyuḷḷatiyē ēṉ iṅkut tiruppik kūṟavēṇṭu meṉpatu teḷivā yilli. orukāl, aḷapeṭi uyirneṭiliṉ, puṇariyal vēṟupāṭāka nikaḻkiṉṟateṉṟu iṅkuk kūṟukiṉṟār pōlum!
maṟuppu
tolkāppiyam iṭikkaḻakak kālattil iyaṉṟa palvēṟu taṉittamiḻ muṉṉūlkaḷiṉiṉṟu tokutta tokic cārpu nūlātaliṉ, vaṭamoḻip puluta aṟivikkoṇṭu.
mūvaḷa picittal ōreḻut tiṉṟē	(eḻuttu. 5)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉār eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu, kūṟṟuppōṟ koṭiyatē.
eḻutteṉappaṭupa.......muppaḵteṉpa.”	(eḻuttu. 1)

ā ī  ū ē  ai ōōḷa.....neṭṭeḻut teṉpa.	(eḻuttu. 4)nīṭṭamvēṇṭiṉ....... eṉmaṉār pulavar. 	(eḻuttu. 6)
eṉṟu palarpālilēyē tamiḻamuṉṉūlārit tolkāppiyar kuṟit tiruttal kāṇka.
īraḷapicittalōreḻuttiṉṟē”eṉṟuvariyaṟuttataṉāl,ataṟkuvilakkākavarumciṟupāṉmanikaḻcciyāṉaaḷapeṭiyamāṇavaṉaṟitaṟporuṭṭu,ataṉiyaṭuttu,nīṭṭamvēṇṭiṉ..... eṉmaṉār pulavar, eṉṟu potuppaṭac curukkik kūṟiṉār. āyiṉ, uyiraḷa peṭi eṉṟu evvāṟu  nikaḻumeṉṟum, ataṟku aṭiyāḷam etuveṉ ṟum, ettaṉi māttiriyeṉṟum, virivākat teriyavēṇṭutaliṉ, avaṟṟi iṅku moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiṉār. eḻuttiṉ taṉinili paṟṟiyatu eḻuttiyal; ataṉ coṉṉili paṟṟiyatu moḻiyiyal.
ōreḻuttuc collāyiṉ, taṉiniliyilum iruvaki vaḻakkilum aḷapeṭi nikaḻum.
e-kā : 	ēeee aṇṇā!-ulakiyalaḷapeṭi
	ōo iṉitē (kuṟaḷ. 1176) - ceyyuḷiyalaḷapeṭi
kūṟṟu
oliyiyaṟ collip piṉṉar āyattoṭaṅki ōreḻuttuc col, īreḻuttuc col, mūveḻuttuc col eṉa ati mūvakiyāka vakukkiṉṟār. ataṉpiṉ mutaleḻuttukaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟār. makarak kuṟukkam mīṇṭum orumuṟi collappaṭukiṉṟatu. aikārak kuṟukkamum collappaṭukiṉṟatu. mūṉṟiṟku  mēṟpaṭṭa puṇariyal eḻuttu vēṟupāṭu kaḷi avar oppukkoḷḷāviṭiṉ, ik kuṟukkaṅkaḷi ivviṭattiṟ kūṟuvatu poruntātu. piṟkālattār aikāra aukāraṅkaḷi neṭilkaḷākac ceyyuḷil āḷvataṉ iṭarppāṭṭi uṇarnta pōtu, pōlik koḷkiyip pukuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
maṟuppu
makarakkuṟukkatti moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiyatu, kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamaṉṟu. nūṉmarapil eḻuttukaḷiṉ māttiri yaḷavik kūṟumiṭattu, makarakkuṟukka māttiri kāleṉṟu kūṟa nērntatu. moḻimarapil makarak kuṟukkam nikaḻum iṭattic colla vēṇṭiyiruntataṉāl,


ceyyu ḷiṟutip  pōli  moḻivayiṉ
ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum 			(18)
ṉakāri muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum 			(19)
eṉṟār āciriyar. īriṭattum kūṟavēṇṭiyatu kūṟalēyaṉṟik kūṟiyatu kūṟalaṉṟu. 
ariyaḷapu kuṟukal makara muṭittē
iciyiṭaṉ arukum teriyuṅ kāli 	(nūṉ. 13)
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē	(nūṉ. 14)
eṉṉum iv viru nūṟpākkaḷiyum, mēṟkūṟiya moḻimarapu nūṟpākka ḷuṭaṉ oppunōkki uṇmi kāṇka.
tolkāppiyattiṉ muṉṉūlkaḷāṉa taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷellām, iyalpāṉa tamiḻeḻuttukaḷi mottam muppateṉṟē koṇṭu, avaṟṟuḷ nāṉkiṉ iṭavēṟupāṭṭālum puṇarcci viḷivālum ēṟpaṭṭa mut tiripolikaḷic cārpeḻuttukaḷeṉṟum, iyalpāṉa mūlamuppati yum mutaleḻuttukaḷeṉṟum, vēṟupaṭutti vaḻaṅkiṉum, mutaleḻuttu kaḷiyē mutaṉmiyākak karutiṉaveṉpatu,
eḻutteṉap paṭupa
akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy
muppaḵ teṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
eṉṉum tolkāppiya mutal nūṟpāvāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
ellā meyyoṭuṅ kūṭā īruyirum uyirēṟā oru meyyumāṉa muccārpeḻuttukaḷuḷ iraṇṭu caṟṟuk kuṟukiyolippiṉum, olittiripē cārpeḻuttukaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpām. piṟkālap patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa naṉṉūlār, olik kuṟukkamum nīṭṭamum kalappumāṉa iyalpukaḷiyuñ cērttu, cārpeḻuttup patteṉa virittār. ik          koḷki tolkāppiyarukkilli. āyiṉ, olittiripu pōṉṟē olikkuṟukka nīṭṭamum coṉṉilik kuriyavātaliṉ, avaṟṟi moḻi marapiṟ kūṟiṉār. ātalāl, makarak kuṟukkamum aikārak kuṟukkamum kūṟuvataṟku moḻimarapē takka iyalām.

aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa	(1091)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvil, aiyar eṉṉum vaṭivamē toṉṟutoṭṭu mūlattilum uriyilum vaḻaṅkivarutal kāṇka.
kūṟṟu
yakarattiṉ maṭṭiṭṭa vaḻakku vēṟōriṭattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cakaramey akarattoṭum aikāra aukāraṅkaḷōṭum kūṭi varuvatilli. akarattoṭu varāmikkuk  kāraṇam, aṇṇameyyākiya cakarattiṉ piṉvarum akaram aṇṇavuyirākiya ekaramākat tirintuviṭuvatē. itu tolkāppiyar kāla moḻiniliyik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. an nili pala cakara mutaṟcoṟkaḷ vantuḷḷa kaḻaka ilakkiyak kāla niliyiṉiṉṟu vēṟu paṭṭatāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
maṟuppu
tolkāppiyar kālattiṟ cakara mutaṟcol oṉṟēṉum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkavilliyeṉpatu caṟṟum poruntātu. cakkaram, cakaṭam mutaliya orucila (vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyākiya) irumoḻip potuc coṟkaḷi nīkkiṉum, peruvāri ulaka vaḻakkum iṉṟiyamiyā aṭippaṭiyumāṉa cakku, cakki, cakati, caṅku, caccaravu, caṭṭam, caṭṭi, caṭṭu, caṭṭi, caṭaṅku, caṭār, caṭi, caṭivu, caṇṇu, caṇpu, caṇṭi, caṇṭu, caṇṭi, caṇāy, catakku, catuppu, cati, cantu, canti, cappaṭṭi, cappaḷi, cappāṇi, cappu, cappi, campaḷam, campā, cammaṇam, camaṭṭi, camam, camaḻ, cami, car, caracara, caraṭṭu, caraḷ, cari, caruku, caruvu, carēl, calli, calakku, calacalappu, calaṅki, calavi, cali, cavvu, cavaṭṭu, cavar, cavaḷi, cavi, caḻakku, caḻi, caḷ, caḷḷi, caḷappu, caḷār, caḷi, caṟṟu, caṟāmpu, caṟukku mutaliya ērāḷamāṉa cakaramutaṟ coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkup piṉṉarē tamiḻil tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatu, eḷḷaḷavum iyaṟkikku ēṟkātu. āriyar vanta kālattiṉiṉṟu āṅkilar vanta kālamvari, tamiḻukkup paṭippaṭiyāka mēṉmēlum kēṭu nērntatēyaṉṟi ākkam nēravilli. tammic cūttirar eṉṟum, tam moḻiyi iḻintār moḻiyeṉṟum tāmē tāḻttikkoṇṭu, āriyari nilattēvareṉṟum avaratu ilakkiyamoḻiyit tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum nampi, iyaṉṟavari tamiḻc colli vaḻakku vīḻttic camaṟkirutac colliyē makiḻcciyōṭum perumitattōṭum vaḻaṅkivanta aṭimit tamiḻar, eṅṅaṉam putut taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷip puṉintiruttal kūṭum?
ākavē, kaṭikkaḻakak kālac cakaramutal tamiḻccoṟka ḷellām āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaṅkālattaṉavē, iti, 
caricamaḻppuc caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi
caḷicakaṭu caṭṭi cavaḷi - cavicaraṭu
cantu cataṅki caḻakkāti yīriṭattum
vantataṉāṟ cammutalum vi
eṉṉum mayilinātaruri mēṟkōḷālum teḷika.
iṉi, tuṭicikiḻār a. citamparaṉār karuttuppaṭi,
cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
aaiau eṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē	(tol. moḻi. 27)
eṉṉum nūṟpāviṉ īṟṟaṭikku, 
avi au eṉṉum oṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē eṉṉum pāṭa vēṟu pāṭu koṇṭu, au eṉṉum ōruyiroḻinta ēṉip patiṉōruyi roṭuṅ kūṭic cakara meyyum coṉmutalām eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉ, cakarak kiḷavit tolkāppiya nūṟpā immiyum mayakkiṟkum maṟuppiṟkum iṭamiṉṟit teḷitalum kāṇka.
kūṟṟu
piṟappiyal
piṟappiyal eḻuttukaḷiṉ olippu muṟiyippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉ iṟuti nūṟpā, avar nōkkup puṟanōkkāka vuḷḷateṉṟum, olippumuṟikku muṉ nikaḻvati vaṇṇikkavilli yeṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu. olippumuṟikku muṟpaṭṭa nilikaḷ paṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷ antaṇar maṟiyil vaṇṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. camaṟkiruta āciriyar koḷkiyi avar aṟintiruntati itu teḷivākak kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
viḷakkam
itu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉṉum uttikku iṉam. eṉṉi? untiyil  eḻunta kāṟṟiṉik kūṟupaṭutti, māttiri kūṭṭikkōṭalum, mūlātāram mutalākak kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟalum vētattiṟku uḷateṉṟu iv vāciriyar kūṟi, avar matampaṟṟi avar koḷvatōr payaṉ iṉṟeṉṟa liṉ, eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟṟu, in nūṟpāviṉ tēvi yiṉmiyiyum payaṉiṉmiyiyumē kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit 
taliyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum niliip 
pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum
aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ
uṟuppuṟ ṟamiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi 
ellā eḻuttum colluṅ kālip
piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
tiṟappaṭat teriyuṅ kāṭci yāṉa	(tol. eḻuttu. 83)
eṉṟu piṟappiyal mutal nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiya mukavuriyē, olippumuṟikku muṟpaṭṭa nikaḻcciyit terintukoḷḷap pōtumāṉatām.
nuṇṇoli (cūkkumi), paruvoli (picanti) iṭiyoli (mattimi), eḻuttoli (vikari) eṉṟu, āriyac civakkoṇmuṭipu kūṟum nālvaki yoliyuḷ, mutal mūṉṟum aṟiviyalōṭu paṭṭaviyalla.
taṉiyeṭuppoliyum mūccoliyum tamiḻukkiṉmiyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ piṟappip paṟṟiyum tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟ kūṟavēṇṭiya tilli.
iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅ
kaṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṉ
kāmak kūṭṭam kāṇuṅ kāli
maṟiyōr tēettu maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ
tuṟiyami nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōr iyalpē      (tol. kaḷavu. 1)
eṉpatu pōṉṟa pala piṟa nūṟpākkaḷ tolkāppiyariṉ camaṟkiruta aṟivit terivikkap pōtumāṉavi.
ātalāl, piṟappiyal iṟuti nūṟpā niṉṟu payaṉiṉmi eṉṉum kuṟṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatē.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' peppiravari cūli 1980
8
`pāṇar kivaḻi'matippuri(maṟuppu)
tiruvāḷar pi. cāmpamūrtti avarkaḷ pāṇar kivaḻiyip paṟṟiya tam matippuriyit toṭaṅku muṉṉarē, kālañceṉṟa maṟittiru vipulānanta aṭikaḷ iyaṟṟiya yāḻnūl matikkoṇāk kaḷañciya meṉṟum, atupaṟṟi iṟṟi yulakam avarkaṭku mikak kaṭamip paṭṭuḷḷateṉṟum, avarkaṭkup pukaḻmāli cūṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. itaṟkuk kāraṇam, an nūliṉ pōli muṭipukaḷellām matippuriyāḷariṉ viruppattiṟ kicintiruttalē.
tamiḻareṉṟu tammic collikkoṇṭu ārāycciyiṉ pērāl tamiḻukkup peruvaci viḷittu āriya ēmāṟṟattiṟku araṇ ceytavaruḷ vipulānanta aṭikaḷum oruvar eṉpatu, iciyiyalpun tamiḻicit taramum aṟiyāta palarkkut teriyātupōyiṉum, avaṟṟi yaṟinta oru cilarkkēṉum teriyātataṉṟu. avarkaḷ uṭal tāṅkiyirunta pōtē kural mattimam eṉṉum avarkaḷ koḷkiyik kaṇṭittuk kural caṭcamē eṉṟu celviyil oru kaṭṭuri varintēṉ. ataṟku avarkaḷ ōr iḷiñar peyarāl maṟuppeḻutiṉārkaḷ. yāṉ am maṟuppiṟ koru maṟuppu varintēṉ. attōṭu niṉṟatu avarkaḷ tarukku. mutaṟkōṇal muṟṟuṅkōṇal ātaliṉ aṭippaṭik koḷkiyē tavaṟāyirukkumpōtu, avarkaḷ nūṉmuṭipu ettakiyatāyirukkum? oru mūli muṭaṅkiyil kūṭṭaṅkūṭṭi, etirppāḷaric celaviṟkup paṇaṅ koṭuttu varavaḻikkātu, icittamiḻ marapaṟiyātār muṉṉili yil avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ yāḻnūli araṅkēṟṟiyatālum; karantit tamiḻc caṅkattār ataṉi veḷiyiṭṭatālum; atu puriyaṟṟa nūlākap pōṟṟappaṭṭuviṭātu. tuvarāṭi yaṇintavar nūlellām tukaḷaṟṟaṉa eṉṉum koḷki emmaṉōrk killi.
iṉi, matippuriyāḷariṉ uṭampaṭāk kūṟṟukaḷi yellām ovvoṉṟākak kūṟi maṟuppām.
kūṟṟukaḷ yāvum iyaṉṟavari curuṅkiya aḷaviṟ kūṟappeṟum.
1. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil vīṇi eṉṉum peyar yāḻiciyōṭu kūṭiya miṭaṟṟiciyiyē kuṟitta tāyiṉ, vīṇi vācippiṉ ceyalmuṟiyum atupaṟṟiya iṭakki valakkit toḻil vēṟupāṭukaḷik kuṟikkum mikap palavākiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷum poyyāyp pōm.
ippōtu vīṇiyeṉa vaḻaṅkum karuvi 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum  13ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil tamiḻnāṭṭilirukkattāṉ  ceytatu. āṉāl, atu toṉṟutoṭṭa muṟippaṭi yāḻ eṉṉum tamiḻp peya rālum ciṟupāṉmi vīṇi eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip peyarālum aḻikkap peṟṟatu. iv vaṭamoḻip peyar kaṭiccaṅka kāla iṟutiyiltāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukuttappaṭṭatu. ataṟkumuṉ vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻil mika arukiyē vaḻaṅkiṉa. vaṭacollē kalavāta taṉittamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya kālamumuṇṭu. atu āriyar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku varumuṉṉatākum.
vīṇi eṉṉum peyar taṉittu varumpōtu, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiyē allatu piṟa yāḻiyē kuṟikkum.
maṅkala miḻappa vīṇi maṇmici
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum,
mācil vīṇiyum māli matiyamum
eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracarum,
kuḻali ṉōci vīṇi monti koṭṭa muḻavatira
eṉṟu tiruñāṉa campantarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
cintāmaṇiyil, kantaruvatattiyā rilampakattil, yāḻ eṉṉum peyarum vīṇi eṉṉum peyarum oruporuṭ kiḷaviyāy oṉṟuk koṉṟu patilāka varukiṉṟaṉa. tēvārattil yāḻ eṉṉum peyar varumiṭat til vīṇi eṉṉum peyarum, vīṇi eṉṉum peyar varumiṭattil yāḻ eṉṉum peyarum vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. nāratayāḻ nāratavīṇi eṉa orē karuvi kuṟittu irupeyar  vaḻakkumuḷḷatu.
vīṇi eṉṉum peyar, yāḻ eṉṉum peyarōṭu  kūṭi varumiṭat til maṭṭum, taṉi miṭaṟṟiciyiyō yāḻiciyoṭu kūṭiya miṭaṟṟici yiyō kuṟikkum. itaṟkuc cintāmaṇi mūlattiṉ uriyiṉiṉṟum pāṇar kivaḻiyāciriyarāl pōtiya  cāṉṟukaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
yāḻ eṉṉum collum, potuppaṭṭa iciyiyum icip pakutiyāṉa paṇṇiyum icikkaruviyāṉa yāḻiyum, iṭattiṟkēṟpak kuṟikkum.
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokii	(tol. akat. 18)
tuṟiyami nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōr iyalpē	(tol. kaḷavu. 1)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyarum,
yāḻōr maṇaviṉik kottaṉaḷ eṉṟē	(maṇimē. 22 : 86)
eṉṟu cīttalic cāttaṉārum, yāḻ eṉṉum colli iciyeṉṉum poruḷil āṇṭaṉar.
pāli kuṟiñci marutañcev vaḻiyeṉa
nālvaki yāḻā nāṟperum paṇṇē
eṉṟu piṅkala muṉivar ac collip paṇ eṉṉum poruḷil āṇṭaṉar.
narappuk karuviyik kuṟittu ac col caṅka nūlkaḷiṟ payiṉṟu varutalāṉum, aṟṟi ulaka vaḻakkilum  ataṟku atuvē poruḷāta lāṉum, ataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ vēṇṭuvatilli.
ākavē, 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil, ikkālattu vīṇiyip peritum otta ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ iruntateṉṟum; ataṟku vīṇiyeṉṉum peyarum ciṟupāṉmi vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉṟum; ap peyar ākupeyarāka miṭaṟṟiciyiyum uṇarttiṉatiṉāl, irupeyarum iṇintu varum iṭikkāla nūṟpakuti kaṭku uriyāciriyar uri varintapōtu, avaṟṟuḷ teṉcollik karuvip peyarākavum vaṭacolli miṭaṟṟici kuṟittatākavum koṇṭaṉar eṉṟum; ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉum vēṟākac ciṟantatoru narappukkaruvi intiyāvilēyē ituvari yiruntatilli yeṉṟum; yāḻ, vīṇi eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum oruporuṭ kiḷaviyāy orē karuviyiyē kuṟikkumeṉṟum; itaṉāl vīṇi vācippuc ceyaṉ muṟiyum  atupaṟṟiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷum poyyāyp pōkā eṉṟum  aṟintukoḷka.
2. yāḻ vaṭivippaṟṟi vipulānanta aṭikaḷ oru vakiyākavum, tiru. varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉār maṟṟoru vakiyākavum, varintuḷḷaṉar.
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻippaṟṟi aṭikaḷ varintu kāṭṭiyuḷḷa paṭamē uṇmiyāṉatāyiṉ vaṟuvāy, kavikkaṭi, unti, oṟṟuṟuppu, tantirikaram, māṭakam, takippu, vaṇar mutaliya uṟuppukaḷellām eṅkē? avaṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum aṭiyārkkunallārum pittarō? aṉṟip poyyarō?
maṟittiru. vipulānanta aṭikaḷ tamiḻicit taratti  aṟiyāta varkaḷ eṉpatu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. atiyē uṇmi yeṉṟu kaṭippiṭikka.
3. uruvaccilic cāṉṟi nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, mēlnāṭṭilum kīḻnāṭṭilum paṇṭik kālattilum iṭik kālattilumirunta yāḻ vilyā ḻottiruntatēyaṉṟi vīṇi yottiruntataṉṟu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. iv viyalpiṟkut teṉṉāṭu vilakkaṉṟu.
paṇṭit tamiḻar iṟṟit tamiḻar pōṉṟavarallar. muṉṉavar nākarikattilum aṟivilum nāyakampeṟṟavar; piṉṉavar aṭimit taṉattilum pētimiyilum peyar peṟṟavar.
‘ceykalik karaci ceyyuḷ’(Poetry is the Queen of Arts) eṉpar cīriyōr. paṇṭit tamiḻar ceyyuṭ kaliyil oppuyarvaṟṟavar eṉpatu ulakaṟinta vuṇmi. veṇpā, āciriyappā, kalippā, vañcippā eṉṉum pākupāṭu tamiḻukkē ciṟantatu. .avaṟṟuḷḷum, veṇpāvum kalippāvum pōṉṟa yāppu vakikaḷi vēṟennāṭṭārum kaṉavilum karutiyirukka muṭiyātu. yāppilakkaṇatti yuḷḷiṭṭa poruḷilakkaṇamō moḻi yilakkaṇattiṉ koṭumuṭiyāyt tamiḻarit taṉivakuppiṉarākak kāṭṭuvatu. ittakiya ilakkaṇattāṟ polivu peṟṟa iyaṟṟamiḻō ṭamiyātu, icināṭakak kalikaḷiyum ataṉoṭu kūṭṭi muttamiḻ eṉa vaḻaṅkiya toṉṟē, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ icippulami yēṟṟattiyum nāṭakappulami nāyakattiyum moḻittiṟa mutaṉmiyiyum matinuṭpa māṇpiyum aiyantiripaṟa viḷakkap pōtiya cāṉṟām. ittaki moḻi vaḷarccikkēṟpa, vāḻkkimuṟi oḻukkam toḻiltiṟam poṟi viṉi mutaliya  piṟa nākarikat tuṟikaḷilum paṇṭit tamiḻar ēṟṟam peṟṟiruntaṉar. ciṟappāka, ceyyuṭkaliyil cīriya pulami vāyntirunta avar, ataṉoṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷa icikkaliyilum ēṟṟam peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatic collavum vēṇṭumō?
paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyam palavakiyil iṟantupaṭṭamiyiyum, taṟkālat tamiḻaṉ kulappiriviṉiyāl tāḻvuṇṭiruppatiyum, intiya varalāṟu talikīḻāy eḻutappaṭṭiruppatiyum, payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭu paṇṭit tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭa yāḻ vilyāḻē eṉpatu naṭunili tiṟampiya nalamil kūṟṟām.
4. tiruñāṉacampantar pāṭiya yāḻmurip paṇṇit  tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar tam yāḻiliṭṭuk  kāṭṭa muṭiyavilli. avyāḻ vīṇiyā yiruntiruntāl aṅṅaṉam tavaṟiyirukka muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉiṉ, miṭaṟṟi ciyiṉ ettiṟa nuṇukkattiyum vīṇiyiṟ kāṭṭa iyalum, ātalāl, 9ām  nūṟṟāṇṭuvari tamiḻakattiliruntatu vilyāḻ vakiyē. ataṉ piṉ (vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu) vīṇi vantatu. atu vantatum yāḻ nekiḻaviṭap paṭṭatu.
tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar, yāḻmurip paṇṇit tam yāḻi liṭṭuk kāṭṭa muṭiyāmikku, mutalāvatu, avaratu aḷaviṟanta civapattiyē kāraṇam. tarumapurattiluḷḷa avaruṭiya uṟaviṉar, avar tiruñāṉacampantariṉ tiruppatika iciyi yāḻppaṭutti vanta tiṟaṉip pukaḻntapōtu, avar ataṟku mika varunti, yāḻiliṭṭuk kāṭṭa muṭiyāta vāṟu tiruppatikam  pāṭumpaṭi tāmē tiruñāṉacampantari vēṇṭiṉār. itaṉāl, yāḻiliṭṭuk kāṭṭa muṭiyāta perumi, tiruppatika icik kiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu avar pēravāk koṇṭami pulaṉākum. ittakip patti nōkkuṭiyavar eṅṅaṉam tam tiṟamiyi aṉṟu kāṭṭiyirukka muṭiyum? avar kāṭṭa muyaṉṟiruppiṉum, avaratu acca maṉappāṉmi eṅṅaṉam iṭantantirukkum?
iraṇṭāvatu, yāḻil maṭṭumaṉṟi vēṟevar miṭaṟṟilum icikka muṭiyātavāṟu, iṟivaṉaruḷāl iṟumpūtuc ceyalāka yāḻmurip paṇṇit tiruñāṉacampantar pāṭiṉāreṉṟu, cēkkiḻār mikat teḷivākak kūṟukiṉṟār. vēṟevar miṭaṟṟiṟkum iyalāta ici yāḻukku maṭṭum eṅṅaṉam  iyaṉṟirukkum? itaṉāl yāḻmurip paṇṇip pāṇar yāḻiliṭṭuk kāṭṭamuṭiyāmi avar yāḻ vilyā ḻottatu eṉṉum pōli yūkippiṟ kētuvākāmi peṟappaṭum.
mēṟkāṭṭiya kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉ uṇmiyip paṭippōr aṟitaṟku, an nikaḻcciyik kūṟum periyapurāṇac ceyyuḷkaḷi īṇṭuk kāṭṭu kiṉṟēṉ:
tarumapuram perumpāṇar tiruttāyar piṟappiṭamām
		ataṉāṟ cāra
varumavartañ cuṟṟattār vantetirkoṇ ṭaṭivaṇaṅki
		vāḻttak kaṇṭu
perumiyuṭip perumpāṇar avarkkurippār piḷḷiyār 				aruḷic ceyta
arumiyuṭip patikantām yāḻiṉāṟ payiṟṟumpē
		ṟaruḷic ceytār.
kiḷiñarumaṟ ṟatukēṭṭuk keḻuvutirup patikattiṟ
		kiḷarnta ōci
aḷavupeṟak karuviyilnīr amittiyaṟṟu mataṉālē
		akila mellām
vaḷarici nikaḻvateṉa viḷamputalum vaḷampukali
		maṉṉar pātam
uḷamnaṭuṅkip paṇintutiru nīlakaṇṭap perumpāṇar
		uṇarttu kiṉṟār.
alakiltirup patikici aḷavupaṭā vakiivarkaḷ
		aṉṟi yēyum
ulakiluḷō runteḷintaṅ kuṇmiyiṉi aṟintuyya
		uṇarttum paṇpāl
palarpukaḻun tiruppatikam pāṭiyaru ḷappeṟṟāl
		paṇpu nīṭi
ilakumici yāḻiṉkaṇ aṭaṅkāmi yāṉkāṭṭap
		peṟuvaṉ eṉṟār.
vētaneṟi vaḷarppavarum viṭiyavarmuṉ toḻututirup
		patikat tuṇmi 
pūtalattōr kaṇṭattum kalattiṉilum nilattunūl 
		pukaṉṟa pēta
nātici muyaṟcikaḷāl aṭaṅkāta vakikāṭṭa
		nāṭṭu kiṉṟār 
mātarmaṭap piṭipāṭi vaṇaṅkiṉār vāṉavarum 
		vaṇaṅki ētta.
vaṇpukali vētiyaṉār mātarmaṭap piṭieṭuttu
		vaṉappiṟ pāṭip
paṇpayilun tirukkaṭikkāp puccātta aṇintuperum
		pāṇa ṉārtām
naṇpuṭiyāḻk karuviyiṉāl muṉpupōl kikkoṇṭu
		naṭattap pukkārk
keṇperukum appatikat ticinarampil iṭaaṭaṅkiṟ
		ṟilli yaṉṟē.
tiruñāṉacampantariṉum mutiyarāṉa tirunāvukkaracar, mācil vīṇiyum eṉṟu pāṭiyiruttalāṉum, tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar yāḻmurip paṇṇi icittaṟkiyalāmipaṟṟit tam yāḻi uṭittaṟ keḻuntapōtu, atit taṭutta campantar `yāḻi viṭṭuviṭṭu vīṇiyi mēṟkoḷḷum eṉṉātu, `tiruppatika iciyi iyaṉṟavari iyakkum eṉṟamiyāṉum; pāṇarum vēṟoru narappuk karuviyi mēṟkoḷ ḷāmiyāṉum avar kiyāṇṭatu vīṇiyotta karuviyē yaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu eṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum.
iṉi, vīṇi ettuṇic ciṟappiṉatāyiṉum, ati iyakkuvōr ettuṇit tiṟavōrāyiruppiṉum, atu evvevar miṭaṟṟiciyiyum iṭṭuk kāṭṭaṟkēṟṟatu eṉpatu muṟṟum  uṇmiyāṉataṉṟu. iṟivaṉ amitta miṭaṟṟiṟkum māntaṉ  amitta vīṇikkum ciṟitēṉum vēṟupāṭu irukkattāṉ ceyyum.
5. yāḻum vīṇiyum vevvēṟu karuvikaḷ
oru narampuḷḷa curiyāḻ mutal āyiram narampuḷḷa ātiyāḻ vari ellā narappuk karuvikaṭkum yāḻ eṉpatu potup peyarākum. evvev vaṭiviliruppiṉum, evvev amippik koṇṭatāyiṉum, evvem muṟiyil icikkappaṭiṉum, evven nāṭṭiṟkuriyatāyiṉum, narampu allatu kampikoṇṭa icikkaruvikaḷellām tamiḻil yāḻ eṉṟē peyar peṟutaṟkuriyaṉa. kiṉṉari allatu cīṟiyāḻ (Fiddle), kittār, curamaṇṭalam, maṇṭoliṉ, pēñcō, pulpultārā, curaket, cittār, kaccuvā, tāvūs, tilrūpā, esrāj, cārintā (cāraṅki), villaṭi, curivīṇi, akappik kiṉṉari, vilyāḻ, tampurā(kēḷviyāḻ), vīṇi mutaliya ellā narappuk karuvikaḷum potuppaṭa yāḻ eṉṟē  aḻikkap peṟuvaṉa. ākavē yāḻ eṉpatu oru taṉippaṭṭa karuviyiṉ ciṟappup peyarākātu. atu tōṟkaruvi tuḷikkaruvi narappukkaruvi kañcakkaruvi eṉṉum nālvakik karuviyuḷ narappukkaruviyi yellām potuppaṭak kuṟikkum potup peyarākum. ip pākupāṭṭiṉpaṭi iṟṟi vīṇiyum oruvaki  yāḻāyirukka, ati maṭṭum eṅṅaṉam vēṟu pirittuk kūṟa muṭiyum?
yāḻum vīṇiyum vevvēṟu karuviyāyiruppiṉ, nāratayāḻ nāratavīṇi eṉac cilappatikārattum, makarayāḻ makaravīṇi eṉac cintāmaṇiyilum, orē karuvi ēṉ iru peyarālum aḻikkappeṟal vēṇṭum? āti (peru), makara, cakōṭa, ceṅkōṭṭu, muṇṭaka, ciṟu mutaliya aṭiyaṭuttālaṉṟi, yāḻ eṉṉum peyar oru ciṟappuvaki narappuk karuviyik kuṟikkātu.  āyiṉum, curukkampaṟṟi, tuppākkiyiṟ palavaki yiruppiṉum avaṟṟuṭ ciṟantatum tuppākkiyeṉṟē aḻikkappeṟutal pōla, yāḻuṭ palavakiyiruppiṉum avaṟṟuṭ ciṟantatum yāḻ eṉṟē  aḻikkappeṟum.

narappukkaruvi vilnāṇoliyiliruntu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatākat teritalāl, vilyāḻē mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiya yāḻvakiyāka  iruttalvēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, pattar (kuṭam) uḷḷa vilyāḻum,  pattarum kōṭum (taṇṭiyum) uḷḷa kōṭṭiyāḻum, pattarum kōṭum tivavumuḷḷa tivaviyāḻum, muṟiyē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. taliccaṅka kālattilēyē tamiḻ muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiyatāl, kaṭiccaṅka kālattuc ciṟanta yāḻvakikaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭa pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṉum nālvaki tivaviyāḻākavē irun tiruttal vēṇṭum. avi muṟiyē yāḻum oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ētēṉu moru vakiyil ciṟantatāyu miruntiruttal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, ceṅ kōṭṭiyāḻ karuviyamippil iṟṟi vīṇiyi ottatākum. narampiṟkup patilāyk kampiyum vārttivaviṟkup patilāy veṇkala meṭṭum tōṟ pōrvikkup patilāy  marappalakiyum vaṟuvāykkup patilāy nuṇ tuḷit takaṭum, koṇṭiruttalē iṟṟi vīṇiyiṉ vēṟupāṭām. kuṭa vaṭiviṟ  ciṟitu māṟutal, kōl eṉappaṭum meṭṭut tokiyiṟ kūṭutal, ākiyavaṟṟiyum vēṟupāṭākac cērttukkoḷḷa lām. iviyellām kālavaḷarcciyil vacatikkēṟpa ēṟpaṭṭavi. īrāyiram āṇṭukku muṟ paṭṭa ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ iṉṟu ittuṇi vēṟupaṭṭiruppatu viyappaṉṟu.
pēriyāḻ piṉṉu makarañ cakōṭamuṭaṉ
cīrpoliyuñ ceṅkōṭu ceppiṉār - tārpolintu
maṉṉun tirumārpa vaṇkūṭaṟ kōmāṉē
piṉṉu muḷavē piṟa
eṉpataṉāl, kaṭiccaṅka kālattil palavaki yāḻkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ nāṉku ciṟantaṉa eṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷḷum ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ taliciṟantatu eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum. kālamuṟippaṭi nōkkiṉ, icivallār kiyāḷum karuvikaḷuḷ pintiṉatu ciṟantirukkumēyaṉṟi  muntiṉatu ciṟantirukkātu. nālvaki yāḻuḷ iṟutiyatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻātaliṉ, atu ēṉiyavaṟṟiṉum ciṟantatāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ākavē, yāḻ eṉpatu tamiḻnāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi ulakameṅkaṇum mukkālum vaḻaṅkum narappuk karuvikaṭkellām potuppeyar eṉṟum,  ac collic ciṟappup peyarākak koṇṭa oru taṉik karuviyum kaṭiccaṅka kālattiruntatilli yeṉṟum, aṉṟirunta palavaki yāḻuḷ taliciṟanta ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻē iṉṟu vīṇi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ peyar koṇṭu vaḻaṅkivarukiṟateṉṟum. yāḻ vēṟu vīṇi vēṟu eṉpatu iru vakiyil tavaṟāṉa tiripukkoḷki eṉṟum aṟintukoḷka. yāḻ eṉṉum teṉcol piṟkālattu vaḻakkaṟṟatiṉālēyē muṟkālattil curiyāḻ eṉa vaḻaṅkiyatu. iṉṟu curivīṇi allatu vīṇi eṉa ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
6. “yāḻil viraluṟukōṭu (Finger-board) illi. ataṉ kuṭatti ṉiṉṟeḻunta narampukaḷ nērē ataṉ vaḷivukkōṭṭil  kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
itaṟku maṟuppu, ēṉi maṟuppukaḷilum, tiru. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉār ilakkiyac cāṉṟum mēṟkōḷum kāṭṭi viḷakkiyuḷḷa ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ 18 uṟuppukaḷiṉ varaṇaṉiyuḷḷum, paṭaṅkaḷuḷḷum kaṇṭu koḷka. 
kōṭē patta rāṇi narampē
māṭaka meṉavarum vakiyiṉa vākum
māṭṭiya pattariṉ vakiyu māṭakamun
tantiri yamitiyuñ cāṟṟiya piṟavu
muntiya nūliṉ muṭinta vakiyē
eṉṉum paḻañ cūttiraṅkaḷiyum,
maṇikaṭi maruppiṉ vāḷār māṭaka vayirat tīntēṉ
aṇipeṟa voḻuki yaṉṉa amiḻtuṟaḻ narampiṉ nalyāḻ	(722)
eṉṉum cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷiyum nōkkuka.
7. yāḻum vīṇiyum iṭikkālattil iṟutivari tamiḻnāṭṭil pakkam pakkamāyiruntu vantaṉa. māṇikkavācakariṉ tiruppaḷḷi yeḻucci yil, iru karuvikaḷum uṭaṉ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. putuk kōṭṭit tirumayattiluḷḷa tirumāl kōviluḷ eḻumuṉivar(captariṣi) ciliyil iru karuvi vaṭivaṅkaḷum uṭaṉ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. vēta ilakkiyat tuḷḷum yāḻum vīṇiyum paṟṟiya kuṟippuḷḷatu.
nālvaki yāḻuḷ ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ ciṟantatātalāṉum,  kulap piriviṉiyāl nērnta iḻivu kāraṇamākap pāṇar tam piḻippiḻantu pāṇtoḻili aṭiyōṭu kiviṭṭamiyāṉum, ēṉi mūṉṟu yāḻkaḷum paṭippaṭiyāy vaḻakkiṟantaṉa.
āriyar teṉṉāṭu varumuṉ allatu vaṭacol tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkūṉṟumuṉ, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ centamiḻp peyarālēyē vaḻaṅki vantatu. ataṉpiṉ, ataṟku vīṇi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ peyar pukuttap paṭṭatu. ciṟitu kālattiṉ piṉ tamiḻp peyar vaḻakkuvīḻntatu. ataṉ piṉṉar, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ vīṇi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ peyarālum, ici yeḷiyār kiyiluḷḷa paṉṉarampu vilyāḻ yāḻ eṉṉum tamiḻp peyarālum, vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. iṭikkālattiṉ piṉ, av vilyāḻum, karuviyum peyarum oruṅku vaḻakkuvīḻntatu. paṇṇicittaṟkuriya paṇṭi yāḻvakikaḷuḷ, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ oṉṟē iṉṟu ciṟitu urukkarantum peyar māṟiyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. tamiḻa nākarikap paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷuḷḷum poruḷkaḷuḷḷum, taliciṟantavaṟṟit taḻuvik koṇṭu avaṟṟit tamavākak kāṭṭuvatu, āriyar vaḻakkam.
ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ vīṇiyeṉap pirikkappaṭṭapiṉ ṉeḻunta nūlkaḷiṟ cila, vilyāḻiyum ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻiyum muṟiyē yāḻum vīṇiyum eṉa uṭaṉ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tirumayam cili ik  koḷki paṟṟiyatē. ennāṭṭilum uyarnta karuviyuṭaṉ tāḻnta karuviyum payilvatu iyalpu. mataṅkam (mirutaṅkam)  irukkumpōtu kañcirāvum, vīṇiyirukkumpōtu pulpultārāvum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. `yāḻ', `vīṇi' eṉṉum peyarkaḷ aṭuttuvarumpōtu, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu miṭaṟṟici yik kuṟiyāviṭiṉ, muṉṉatu vilyāḻiyum piṉṉatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiyumē kuṟikkum.
āriyar ki.mu. 1500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ ciṟuciṟu kuṭumpaṅkaḷākak kuṭiyēṟiviṭṭataṉāl, vēta ilakkiyattil yāḻum  (vilyāḻum) vīṇiyum (ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻum) paṟṟiya kuṟippiruppatu viyappaṉṟu.
mulli nākarika niliyilirunta nāṭōṭikaḷāṉa vēta āriyar intiyāviṟkuḷ pukumuṉṉarē, tamiḻ muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉpatiyum, tamiḻ nākarikam paṉimali (imayam) vari paraviyiruntateṉpatiyum niṉiviliruttutal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭik kālattil yāḻ eṉṉum tamiḻppeyar narappuk karuvi kaḷiyellām kuṟittavaki yoṭṭi, iṉṟu vīṇi eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ peyarum oru kampiyuḷḷa irappōṉ yāḻmutal eḻu kampiyuḷḷa ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ vari ellāk kampi   yicikkaruvikaḷiyum kuṟittu varukiṉṟatu.
ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ vaṭacoṟ peyar pūṇṭapiṉ tamiḻppeyari yiḻantamikku, mutukuṉṟam maṟikkāṭu mutaliya tamiḻnāṭṭūrkaḷ  muṟiyē viruttācalam vētāraṇiyam eṉa vaṭamoḻip peyar pūṇṭapiṉ tam tamiḻp peyarkaḷi yiḻantami ciṟanta oppaṉiyām. av vūrkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭic cērntaṉavāyuḷḷami aṉivarum aṟivar. āyiṉum, avaṟṟi vaṭacoṟ peyarālaṉṟit teṉcoṟ peyarāṟ kuṟippiṉ, iṉṟu pulamakkaḷ tavirap potumakkaḷ poruḷaṟiyār.
8. paṇṭit tamiḻyāḻ vīṇipōṉṟiruntatāyiṉ, ovvoru  yāḻiciñaṉum vilakka vēṇṭiyirunta `atirvu eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟattiṟku eṅṅaṉam kāraṇaṅkāṭṭa muṭiyum? yāḻiṉ melivu narampit teṟitta pōtu, atu ēṉi narappiciyi ṉiṉpam nukarappaṭātavāṟu atircciyi uṇṭupaṇṇiya tātaliṉ, ati  uṭaṉē niṟuttutaṟku yāḻōṉ kiyil oru kōl iruntatu. ik kōluruvam amarāvati yāḻōṉ ciliyil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
pēriyāḻ mutaliya mūṉṟiyāḻkaḷilum, oliperuk kuṟuppākat  tarappuccittāril paṇmoḻi narampiṟkuk kīḻ amittirukkum atirvu narampukaḷippōl, cila atirvu narampukaḷ amikkap peṟṟiruntaṉa. paṇmoḻi narampukaḷi icikkumpōtu, av  atirvu narampukaḷ oli perukkuvatuṭaṉ atirviyum uṇṭupaṇṇum. yāḻ icippavariṉ tērccikkut takkavāṟu av atirvu kuṟiyum, allatu nīṅkum. it takiya atircci allatu iriccal nikaḻātavāṟu, ovvōr yāḻiciñaṉum kāttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. iv vatircci atirvu narampukaḷāl nērntatēyaṉṟi yāḻāl nērntataṉṟu.
iv vatirvu narampukaḷāl iṉimi keṭukiṟateṉ ṟaṟiyappaṭṭapiṉ, pēriyāḻilirunta 14 atirvu narampukaḷ makarayāḻil 12 ākavum cakōṭayāḻil 7 ākavum kuṟikkappaṭṭu, iṟutiyil  ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻil aṭiyōṭu vilakkappaṭṭaṉa.
mēlum, narampiṉ atirvu yāḻiṉ marakkuṟṟattālum piṟakkum. iti, narampiṉiciyāṟ piṟanta pollāmiyāvaṉa:
cempaki yārppuk kūṭa matirvu....
atirveṉap paṭuva tiḻumeṉa liṉṟic
citaṟi yurikkuna ruccarip piciyē 	(cīvaka. 718)
eṉpaṉa.
ivi marak kuṟṟattāṟ piṟakkum:
nīrilē niṟṟal aḻukutal vētal nilamayaṅkum
pārilē niṟṟal iṭivīḻtal nōymarap pāṟpaṭalkōḷ
nērilē cempaki yārppoṭu kūṭam atirvuniṟṟal
cēriṉēr paṇkaḷ niṟamayak kampaṭum ciṟṟiṭiyē 	(cīvaka. 719)
eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyāṉ  aṟika.
yāḻōṉ kiyilirunta kōl, avaṉ taṉ yāḻp pulamiyi araṅkēṟṟiya kālattu avaṉukku aḷikkappaṭṭa varicic ciṉṉamē yaṉṟi, melivu narampatircciyit taṭukkum icikkaruvi yuṟuppaṉṟu. mātavi taṉ āṭalpāṭali araṅkēṟṟit  talikkōṟ paṭṭam peṟṟāḷ. ap paṭṭam oru kōluṭaṉ koṭukkappeṟuvatu. ak kōl talikkōl eṉappaṭum. talikkōl icināṭakakkaliyil talimi kuṟikkum kōl. talikkōl tāṉam, talikkōlācāṉ, talikkōlarivi mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
9. vīṇi orukālum tamiḻnāṭṭi liruntatilliyeṉṟu nūlāciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār.  
ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻiṉum vēṟāka vīṇiyeṉṟoru ciṟanta karuvi orukālum tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntatilli eṉpatu āciriyar karuttēyaṉṟi, vīṇiyē orukālum tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntatilli eṉpataṉṟu.
10. 21 narampuḷḷa pēriyāḻilum paṇmoḻi narampukaḷ ēḻēyākavum ēṉiya vellām atirvu narampukaḷākavu miruntiruppiṉ, ataṟkup perum pattar etaṟku? iciperukka atirvu narampukaḷē pōtumē!
muṟkāla makkaḷ uruvattiṟ periyavarāyiruntatiṉāl, avar kiyāṇṭa karuvikaḷellām periyaṉavākavē yiruntaṉa. makkaḷ uruvam varavarac ciṟuttuvaruvatiṉāl, avarkaḷ kiyāḷum  palavakik karuvikaḷum ciṟuttuvarukiṉṟaṉa. pēriyāḻ mikap paḻiyatātaliṉ, atu mutupaḻaṅkāla makkaṭkēṟṟapaṭi mikap   periyatāyiruntirukkiṉṟatu. atu nālvaki yāḻil mutaṟ kuṟikkappeṟuvataṟku ataṉ paḻimiyē kāraṇam. kaṭiccaṅka kālattilum atu vaḻaṅkiṉatākak kūṟappaṭiṉum, atu uruvattiṟ periyavarkkē uriyatāy arukiṉa vaḻakkākavē vaḻaṅkiṉa tākat terikiṉṟatu.
atirvu narampukaḷāl iciyip perukkalām eṉṟa eṇṇam uṇṭāṉapiṉ, peruṅkalam eṉṉum pēriyāḻil mutaṉmutalāka atirvu narampukaḷ cērkkappaṭṭiruttalvēṇṭum. ākavē, av yāḻil perum pattar muntiṉatum atirvu narampu pintiyatumākum. taliccaṅka kālattu makkaḷ tam uṭaṟ parumaṉukkēṟṟapaṭi, iyalpākavē perum pattar amittiruttal vēṇṭum. piṟkālattuc ciṟṟuruva makkaḷiṉ cīṟiyāḻka ḷōṭu oppunōkkiya piṉṉarē, ataṟkup peruṅkalam allatu pēriyāḻ eṉṟu perumiccol aṭikoṭuttup piṉṉōr peyariṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. mutaṟkālattil atu aḷavāṉa karuvi yākavē karutappaṭṭirukkalām. paḻaṅkālattiṟ peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkiya tāka nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum mattaḷam, ataṉ parumi kāraṇamāka iṉṟu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭāmal cila ciṟṟūrkaḷiṟ patuṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟatu. ataṉ ciṟuvaṭivāṉa mataṅkam (mirutaṅkam) iṉṟu peruvaḻakkāyuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉamē, pēriyāḻ cīṟiyāḻ nilimikaḷum kaṭiccaṅka kālattil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
pēriyāḻiṉum pēriyāḻāṉa ātiyāḻ (peruṅkalam) vēṟu;  nāratappēriyāḻ eṉpatum vēṟu.
peruṅkalamāvatu pēriyāḻ; atu kōṭṭiṉataḷavu paṉṉiru cāṇum, vaṇaraḷavu cāṇum, pattaraḷavu paṉṉiru cāṇum, ip peṟṟik kēṟṟa āṇikaḷum tivavum untiyum peṟṟu, āyiraṅkōl koṭut tiyalvatu; eṉṉi?
āyira narampiṟ ṟātiyā ḻākum
ēṉiyuṟuppu moppaṉa koḷalē
pattaraḷavuṅ kōṭṭiṉa taḷavum
otta veṉpa irumūṉ ṟiraṭṭi
vaṇarcāṇoḻitteṉa vittaṉar pulavar
eṉpatu aṭiyārkkunallār urippāyiram. nāratap pēriyāḻ vilyāḻ vakiyākum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, tirumāṉ varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉār  avarkaḷiṉ pāṇarkivaḻi muṭipukaḷ aṉittum uṇmiyē eṉṟum, ataṟkut tirumāṉ cāmpamūrtti avarkaḷ varinta  maṟuppu caṟṟum poruntāteṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
kuṟippu: vīṇi eṉṉum peyar, vaṭacol eṉa im maṟuppuriyiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷēṉ. atu viṇ eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟanta teṉ  collē yeṉṟu mūtaṟiñar tuṭicikiḻār kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. viṇṇeṉa olittatu, viṇṇeṉa icittatu, viṇṇeṉa irintatu, viṇṇeṉat teṟittatu eṉa iruvaki vaḻakkiṉum vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, viḷ eṉṉum vērccoṟku olittal collutal eṉṉum poruḷka ḷuṇmiyāṉum, avarkaḷ kūṟṟum oppukkoḷḷat takkatē.
iṉi, mukavīṇi eṉa oru kuḻaṟkaruvi yiruttalāṉum, ataṉ ici oruvakiyil vīḷiyi(cīḻkki yoliyi) yottiruttalāṉum, vīṇi eṉṉum peyar miṭaṟṟiciyiyuṅ kuṟittalāṉum, vīḷi yeṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu vīṇi yeṉṉum peyar tirintatō eṉavum aiyuṟak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
viṇ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu nīṭṭal tiripākavō, vīḷi yeṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟu pōlittiripākavō, vīṇi yeṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiyirukkakkūṭiyatu poruttamē. oru poruḷpaṟṟit teṉ colliṉ vēṟāka vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟ cila, teṉcolli ṉaṭiyākavē piṟantiruttaliṉ, mēṟkūṟiya kūṟṟu viyappiṟkiṭamāṉataṉṟu. 
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' caṉavari 1951
9
collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷ
matippuri maṟuppu
īrāṇṭukaṭkumuṉ eṉ collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷip  paṟṟi intut tāḷil oru maruṅku pōṟṟiyum oru maruṅku tūṟṟiyum  oru matippuri vantiruntatu. ati eṉakku oru naṇpar koṇṭuvantu kāṭṭiṉār. ataṉ piṉṉarttāṉ,  collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷ oru paṭi intuc ceytit  tāṭkut tiruvāḷar va. cuppiyāp piḷḷiyavar kaḷāl matippurikkāka viṭukkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉa ūkippāl aṟiya nērntatu. eṉakkuc collāmal viṭuttatiṉāl ati muṉṉaṟiya villi. eṉṉik kēṭṭiruntāl nāṉ taṭuttiruppēṉ. ēṉeṉil, viḷivu muṉterintatu eṉpatu maṭṭumaṉṟu, eṉ collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷi āyum āṟṟaluṭiyār intu niliyattilāvatu an niliyac cārpilāvatu illi eṉpatē eṉ karuttu.
matippuriyip paṭittavuṭaṉ tiruvāḷar cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ koḷkikkuc cārpāṉa allatu māṟallāta pakutiyip pōṟṟiyum māṟāṉa pakutiyit tūṟṟiyum varintuḷḷārkaḷ, eṉṟu eṉ naṇpariṭam coṉṉēṉ. avar uṭaṉē oru maṟuppuri viṭukka vēṇṭum eṉṟār. nāṉ am matippuriyip poruṭpaṭuttātatiṉāl avar viruppiṟkiciyātu vāḷā iruntuviṭṭēṉ.
iṉṟu, eṉ pāṇar kivaḻi matippuri maṟuppip pārttavuṭaṉ,  eṉ naṇpar mīṇṭum eṉṉiṭam ōṭivantu, aiyā! ēṉiyar iyaṟṟiya nūlpaṟṟi yeḻunta matippurikku maṭṭum maṟuppuri viṭuttīrkaḷē! ēṉ tāṅkaḷ iyaṟṟiya `collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷ' matippurikku iṉṉum maṟuppuri viṭukkavilli? itaṉāl, tiruvāḷar cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyā ravarkaḷ varinta matippuri cariyāṉatē yeṉṟum, taṅkaṭku ati maṟukkum matuki yilli eṉṟum allavā paṭum?  eṉṟu eṉakku māṉa vuṇarcci tōṉṟumāṟu oru muṟi kūṟiyatōṭamiyātu tirumpat tirumpac colli vaṟpuṟuttiyatiṉ payaṉāka, im maṟuppuri varukiṉṟa teṉka.
cāttiriyār avarkaḷ kūṟṟukaḷi em vaḻakkampōl ovvoṉṟā yeṭuttu maṟuppām:
1. antaṇar eṉpār oru kulattār eṉpatu tolkāppiyat tilēyē kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
antaṇar eṉṉum peyar mutalāvatu muṉivari allatu tuṟavōriyē kuṟittateṉa, ac collamippē kāṭṭum.
antaṇmiyuṭiyār antaṇar. antaṇmi aḻakiya aruḷu ṭimi, ātaliṉ, antaṇarāvār aḻakiya aruḷuṭiya muṉivar.
antaṇmipūṇṭa.... antaṇar.”	(tirumantiram 234)
antaṇ potiyi lakattiyaṉār	 (nakkīrar)
(potiyil eṉpatu iṅku iṭippiṟavaral)
antaṇa reṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkuñ
centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ	(kuṟaḷ. 30)
eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar teḷḷat teḷiyap poruḷuṇarttiyatumaṉṟi, ik kuṟaḷit tuṟavōrip paṟṟik kūṟum nīttār perumi yatikārattil vittatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
piṅkalattiṉ, kuravarip paṟṟik kūṟum aiyar vakiyil,
muṉivar mātavar iliṅkikaḷ muṉivar
paṭivar uṟuvar paṇṇavar aiyar
aṟavōr tapōtaṉar aṟiñar antaṇar
tuṟavōr kaṭintōr mōṉiyar yōkiyar
kōpaṅ kāyntōr nīttōr meyyar
tāpatar iruṭikaḷ tampeya rākum
eṉa iruṭikaḷ (muṉivar) peyarik kūṟum mutaṟ cūttirattil antaṇar eṉṉum peyar kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tuṟaviyar ciṟanta aṟavōrum tūyōrumātaliṉ antaṇaṉ eṉṉum peyar aṟavōṉ tūyōṉiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
mutaṉmutalāka makkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭa aruḷuṭiyōr muṉivarē yāyiṉum uṇmiyil iṟivaṉē ciṟanta aruḷuṭi yōṉātaliṉ, antaṇaṉ eṉum pēr iṟivaṉiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
maṇimiṭaṟ ṟantaṇaṉ 	(akam. kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu)
centī vaṇṇa ranta ṇāḷar	(tēvāram 1816)
uṟṟanōy nōṉṟal uyirkkuṟukaṇ ceyyāmi
aṟṟē tavattiṟ kuru.	(kuṟaḷ. 261)
taṉṉūṉ perukkaṟkut tāṉpiṟi tūṉuṇpāṉ
eṅṅaṉam āḷum aruḷ.	(kuṟaḷ. 251)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷkaḷālum, aruḷuṭimi, `pulāṉ maṟuttal, kol lāmi eṉṉum aṟaṅkaḷi vaḷḷuvar tuṟavaṟaviyalil vittu vaṟpuṟuttuvatālum, tuṟavaṟattiṉ iyalpālum, antaṇar eṉṉum peyar tuṟaviyarkkē poruntum eṉpatu tēṟṟam.
tolkāppiyar, tuṟaviyari antaṇar aṟivar aiyar tāpatar eṉṉum coṟkaḷālum, kalvit toḻilāl avarō ṭorumaruṅkop puṭiya illaṟattārip pārppār eṉṉum collālum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
nūlē karakam mukkōl maṇiyē
āyuṅ kāli antaṇark kuriya	(1570)
eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟum antaṇar ciṉṉaṅkaḷē, avar tuṟaviyaric cuṭṭik kūṟiyamiyi yuṇarttum.
antaṇar eṉṉum peyar ivvāṟu tuṟaviyarkkē yuriyatāyiṉum poruḷilakkaṇattiṉ  poruṭṭup palvēṟu toḻilkaḷiyum kalvi pōr vaṇikam kittoḻil eṉṉum nālvakiyuḷ aṭakki ellāk kulaṅkaḷiyum nāṟpāṟpaṭuttuk kūṟumpōtu.
orupeyarp potuccol uḷporu ḷoḻiyat 
teripuvēṟu kiḷattal talimiyum paṉmiyum
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉum aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉum,	(tol. 532)
eṉṉum vitippaṭi 
uvaccar, pūcāriyar, tēvarāḷar, kurukkaḷ, paṇṭāravar, pulavar, kaṇakkāyar, kaṇiyar, vaḷḷuvar, pārppār mutaliya paltiṟak kalvit toḻi lāḷarum antaṇar eṉṉum talimic  collāṟ kuṟikkap peṟuvar, allatu antaṇaruḷ aṭakkap peṟuvar. (iṅkuk kuṟikkap peṟṟā rellārun tamiḻk kulattārē).
itaṉāl, antaṇar eṉṉum peyar ciṟupāṉmi illaṟattārāṉa kalvit toḻilāḷariyuṅ kuṟikkum. iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟippatu nūl (ceyyuḷ) vaḻakkēyaṉṟi ulaka vaḻakkaṉṟu.
āriya iṉattic cērnta pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu (tamiḻakam) vantu kuṭiyēṟiyapōtu, avaruḷ tuṟavu allatu tuṟavotta  niliyiluḷḷār antaṇar eṉṟum, illaṟattār pārppā reṉṟum, niliyoppumiyum toḻiloppumiyum paṟṟi aḻikkappaṭṭār. piṉpu, iṉavorumi yālum āriya  varuṇācirama tarumam paṟṟiya kulappiriviṉiyālum, iru cār pirāmaṇarum oru cārarāki, antaṇar pārppār eṉa nūl vaḻakkilum, pārppār eṉa maṭṭum ulakavaḻakkilum, aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. pirāmaṇari maṭṭum antaṇar eṉak kūṟum tamiḻnūlka ḷellām piṟkālattaṉavaṉṟi muṟkālattaṉavalla. antaṇar pārppār eṉṉum irupeyarum tūya teṉcollāyiruttalē, ap peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa tamiḻ vakuppār allatu kulattār toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭiliruntu vantamiyip pulappaṭuttum. mēṉāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta airōppiyar (āṭcit toḻil paṟṟit) turimār eṉṉum tamiḻ allatu teluṅkac collāṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭatu pōṉṟē, vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta pirāmaṇarum antaṇar allatu pārppār eṉṉum tamiḻccollāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉar eṉka. āyiṉum, pirāmaṇar tammit tamiḻariṉum uyarntavarākak kāṭṭik koḷvatāl,  tammip pirāmaṇar eṉṉum vaṭa collālaṉṟip pārppār eṉṉum teṉcollāṟ kuṟikka virumpukiṉṟilar. āyiṉ, tamiḻnāṭṭil ellāvakiyilum talimi peṟṟa muṉivarik kuṟikkum aiyar antaṇar eṉṉum  teṉcoṟkaḷāl tammik kuṟippatil perumiyum peru makiḻvuṅ koḷkiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu, av viru teṉcoṟkaḷiyum vaṭacollākak kāṭṭavum muyalkiṉṟaṉar. im muyaṟciyiṉ nōkkam, pirāmaṇarē toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭil aiyareṉṟum antaṇa reṉṟum aḻikkappeṟṟu vantavar eṉak kāṭṭuvatēyākum. itaṉāl. tamiḻ nākarikam āriya nākarikamākak koḷḷappaṭṭuviṭum eṉpatu karuttu.
pirāmaṇar vantapōtirunta tamiḻap pārppār pirāmaṇaroṭu kalantuviṭṭamiyāl, iṉṟu pārppār eṉṟu (vaṭamoḻiyaṟiyātu tamiḻi lēyē vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟum) oru  tamiḻak kulattār illi. iṅṅaṉaṅ kalantu pōṉamiyāl,  pirāmaṇarukkut tokip perukkamum tamiḻp pārp pārkkuk kulavuyarvum payaṉ. kurukkaḷ eṉṉum orucār tamiḻap pūcaṉik kulattāruḷ oru pakutiyiṉar, aṇmiyilēyē vaṭa moḻiyik kaṟṟuk koṇṭu atil vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟuvataṉ payaṉākap pirāmaṇarākiyuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. tūya tamiḻk kulattārāṉa ceṭṭimār tammi viciyareṉṟum, nāṭārkaḷ tammic cattiriyareṉṟum, vēḷāḷar tammic cūttirar allatu caṟcūttirar eṉṟum, collikkoḷvatu mēṟkūṟiyati naṉku viḷakkum.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, antaṇar eṉṉum col muṉivariyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkumeṉṟum, poruḷilakkaṇap pākupāṭupaṟṟic ciṟupāṉmi kalvit toḻilip potuvāka vuṭiya palkulattāriyum cuṭṭumeṉṟum, oru niliyāriyaṉṟi oru kulattāriyun taṉippaṭac cuṭṭāteṉṟum, pirāmaṇari antaṇareṉṟatu piṟkāla vaḻakkeṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.
2. pattiṉi eṉṉum col patnī eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripaṉṟi, pattaṉ (paktaṉ) eṉpataṉ peṇpāl vaṭivamaṉṟu.
vaṭamoḻiyil kaṇavaṉik kuṟikkum pati eṉṉum colliṉ peṇpāl vaṭivamāṉa patnī eṉpatu, maṉivi eṉṟu  maṭṭum poruḷ paṭuvatēyaṉṟit tamiḻiṟpōl kaṟpuṭiya  maṉiviyiyē kuṟippataṉṟu. pattiṉi allatu pattiṉippeṇ eṉa oru peṇṇik kuṟittut tamiḻar collumpōtellām, kaṇavaṉmāṭṭup patti yuṭiyavaḷ eṉṟē karutic colkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatic cāttiriyār avarkaḷ aṟiyātiruppatu varuntattakkatu. pattiṉi eṉṉum col maṉivi eṉṟē poruḷpaṭu māyiṉ, pattiṉippeṇ (maṉivippeṇ) eṉṉum irupeyaroṭṭu vaḻakkiṟkē iṭamirātu. it toṭaril, pattiṉi eṉpatu ciṟappup peyarum peṇ eṉpatu  potup peyarumākum. teyvattiṉiṭattum teyvat taṉmiyuḷḷa aracaṉ āciriyaṉ mutaliyōriṭattum kāṭṭum accat tōṭu kūṭiya aṉpu patti eṉappaṭum. pattiyuṭiyavaṉ pattaṉ. pattiyuṭiyavaḷ pattiṉi.
kulamakaṭ kaḻakutaṉ koḻunaṉip pēṇutal, kaṟpuṭi maṉivikkuk kaṇavaṉē teyvam  eṉṉum paṇṭik koḷkipaṟṟi, ciṟanta maṉivi kaṇavaṉiṭattē pattipūṇ ṭoḻukiṉamiyāl, pattiṉi eṉappaṭṭāḷ. ākavē, pattaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ peyar teyvapatti yuṭiyavaṉiyum, pattiṉi eṉṉum peṇpāṟ peyar kaṇavaṉ pattiyuṭiyavaḷiyum kuṟippaṉavākum.
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyum maḻi	(kuṟaḷ. 55)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷum, pattiṉi eṉṉum peyark kāraṇatti ōraḷavu uṇarttum.
iṅṅaṉam, pattiṉi eṉṉum col pattiyuṭiyāḷ eṉṟu pakucoṟ poruḷ koṇṭiruppavum, atip patnī  eṉṉum vaṭacollō ṭicittataṟku, patti eṉṉum col pakti eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripeṉac cāttiriyār avarkaḷ koṇṭamiyē kāraṇamākum.
patti eṉṉum col, paṟṟu eṉpataṉ maṟuvaṭivāṉa pattu eṉpata ṉiṉṟu tirinta tūya teṉcollākum.
ṟakaraviraṭṭi takaraviraṭṭiyākat tirivatu iyalpu.
e-kā : oṟṟu - ottu; kuṟṟu - kuttu.
tañci māvaṭṭattil poṟkollarip pattar eṉpatu marapu. orukāl, poṉṉi neruppiṟ paṟṟavippavar eṉpatu ap peyarp poruḷāyirukkalām.
pattuṭi yaṭiyavark keḷiyavaṉ eṉṉum tivviyap paṉuval (tiruvāymoḻi 1:3:1) toṭaril, pattu eṉṉum col patti eṉap poruḷpaṭṭatu kāṇka.
vayalik kuṟikkum paṟṟu eṉṉum collum pattu eṉat tirin tiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. pattil āṟupōṉāl eṉṉa vākum? eṉpatu oru viṭukati.
paṟṟu eṉṉum col pattiyi allatu aṉpik kuṟippatip paṟṟi oṉṟum kūṟavēṇṭuvatilli.
paṟṟuka paṟṟaṟṟāṉ paṟṟiṉi yappaṟṟip
paṟṟuka paṟṟu viṭaṟku.	(kuṟaḷ. 350)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷē pōtiyatākum.
kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟu ikaravīṟākat tirivatu peruvaḻakku.
e-kā : kuccu - kucci; kuñcu - kuñci; pañcu - pañci; muṭṭu - muṭṭi
ivvāṟē, pattu eṉṉuñ collum patti eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
paḻimiyāṉa teṉcoṟkaṭkellām teṉmoḻi vaḻiyākap poruḷkoḷḷutal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyākap poruḷ koḷḷutal kūṭātu. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷvār iruṭṭuvīṭṭil kuruṭ ṭerumipōl iṭarppaṭuvatu tiṇṇam.
pattiṉi eṉṉum collip patnī eṉṉum collō ṭiṇittata ṉālēyē, muṉṉataṉ pakuti pattu allatu patti eṉpati uṇara muṭiyāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu. aṅṅaṉam uṇara muṭiyāmiyiṉālēyē, pattaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ peyar ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappeṟavilli.
nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭamoḻiyil koṇṭu avaṟṟi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvatum, tūya teṉcoṟkaṭkum oli yoppumipaṟṟi vaṭacoṉmūlaṅ kāṭṭuvatum, ettuṇi nāṭkuc cellum?
3. eḻuttu eṉpataṉ pakuti eḻu eṉpatēyaṉṟi eḻutu eṉpataṉṟu eṉpatu tolkāppiyattālaṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
eḻuttu eṉpataṉ pakuti eḻu eṉpatē eṉṟu tolkāppiyar ōriṭattuṅ kūṟavilli. eḻuttoliyuṇṭāvatik kuṟikkumiṭattu, eḻutal eḻuvutal mutaliya coṟkaḷi āḷkiṉṟārēyaṉṟi eḻutu vatik kuṟikkumiṭattaṉṟu. karutu eṉṉum col karuttu eṉa iraṭṭittut toḻiṟpeyarum toḻilāku peyarumāvatupōl, eḻutu eṉṉum collum eḻuttu eṉa iraṭṭittut toḻiṟpeyarum toḻilāku peyarumākum. varivaṭivu olivaṭivik kuṟippataṉālum, payiṟci         mikutiyāl atu olivaṭivō ṭoṉṟiyatu pōlat tōṉṟuvatālum, eḻuttu eṉṉum col varivaṭivik  kuṟippatuṭaṉ ākupeyar muṟiyil olivaṭiviyuṅ kuṟikkum. iv volivaṭivup poruḷil aḵtu irumaṭiyāku peyarākum.
ōviya varivik kuṟikkum eḻuttu (eḻututal) eṉṉum collē, varivariviyuṅ kuṟikkum, varivaṭivum oruvaki yōviyamātaliṉ, eḻututal varital.
“Letter. Fr. lettre, L. litera, from lino. litum, to besmear, an early mode of writing being by graving the characters upon tablets smeared over with wax, eṉṟu The Imperial Dictionary eṉṉum āṅkila akarātiyiṟ kuṟittiruppatu oppu nōkkattakkatu.
eḻu eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu eḻum toḻiṟpeyar, eḻal, eḻāl, eḻil, eḻutal, eḻuki, eḻūu  eṉa irukkumēyaṉṟi, eḻuttu eṉa irukkātu. ākavē, avali niṉittukkoṇṭu urali iṭippatupōla, oliyi niṉittukkoṇṭu variyik kuṟikkum peyarkkup pakuti kūṟuvatu poruntātu.
4. teṉcoṟkaṭkup poruḷ viḷakkavanta puttakattiṟ cila vaṭa coṟkaḷum teṉcoṟkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vaṭacoṟkaḷivi, teṉcoṟkaḷivi yeṉa yāṉ aṟiyāma lilli. cila vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻil vaḻakkūṉṟi yuḷḷami kāraṇamākat taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi avaṟṟit toṭarpuṭiya teṉcoṟkaḷoṭu cērttuk kūṟiyirukkiṉṟēṉ. aṅṅaṉam kūṟap paṭṭavi iraṇṭoṉṟē. avaṟṟi vaṭacolleṉa vitantu kuṟittu mirukkiṉṟēṉ. aṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟikkappaṭātavi yellām teṉcol lākavē yirukkum. avaṟṟi yellām allatu avaṟṟuḷ oru pakutiyic cāttiriyār avarkaḷ vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkiyiruppārkaḷā yiṉ, atu avarkaḷatu kuṟṟamēyaṉṟi eṉ kuṟṟamaṉṟu.
5.  āciriyar moḻiyoli nūl (Phonology), colvaṭivu nūl (Morphology), poruṭpāṭṭu nūl (Semasiology), ākiyavaṟṟip payiṉṟiruntāl naṉṟāyiruntirukkum.
kaṭanta irupāṉāṇṭukaḷāka moḻinūlil mūḻkik kiṭanta  eṉakku, mīṉkuñcukku nīccup payiṟṟuvatupōlum, kol teruvil kuṇṭūci viṟpatupōlum, cāttiriyār avarkaḷ  moḻinūl tuṟikaḷi yuṇartta virumpiyatu, mika viyappi viḷikkiṉṟatu.
pārppāṉ tamiḻum vēḷāḷaṉ kirantamum vaḻavaḻā koḻakoḻā  eṉpatu orupuṟamirukka, vaṭamoḻiyi iyaṉmoḻiyākavō, tamiḻukku muntiya moḻiyākavō koḷpava rellām, oppiyaṉ moḻinūl māṇavarātaṟkum uriyavarallar eṉpatu eṉatu āynta muṭipu. ceyaṟki valloliyellām ciṟantu, palukka (uccarikka) aritāṉa collamip pellām peṟṟu, āciyā, airōppā ākiya iruperuṅ kaṇṭaṅkaṭkup potuvāṉa coṟkaḷiyellām taḻuvi, eṇṇaru nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka iṭukuṟimuṟiyil vaḷampaṭuttappaṭṭa vaṭamoḻiyi; iyalpāyeḻuntu, eḷiyavoli koṇṭu iḷañciṟārum olikkum collamippup peṟṟu, māntaṉ tōṉṟiya kālantoṭṭu māṇpāka vaḷarkkappeṟṟa tamiḻukku muntiyatākavum mūlamākavuṅ kāṭṭat tuṇivār; moḻinūṟṟuṟikaḷil muṟṟat tuṟipōya mutuperum pulavarākat tammit tāmē matittut tarukkiṉ, avarukku viṭiyāka yāṉoṉṟum viṭuttaṟkilli.
iṉi, eṉ `collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷil' cāttiyār avarkaḷ kuṟippiṭāta iraṇṭoru kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷ uḷa. avi coṟporuḷ paṟṟiyavi. avaṟṟi aṭutta patippil tiruttik koḷvēṉ.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' cūṉ 1951

moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar 
vāḻkkic cuvaṭukaḷ
ti.pi. 1933 (1902)	:	tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam, caṅkaranayiṉār kōyilil cuṟavam - 26ām nāḷ (7.2.1902) piṟantār. 
tanti	:	ñāṉamuttu
tāy	:	paripūraṇam
ti.pi. 1938 (1907)	:	vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam, āmpūr micuri
toṭakkakkalvi	:	nallañcal uluttariṉ viṭiyūḻiya naṭunilip paḷḷi.
uyarnilikkalvi:	tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam, pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi, tiruccapi viṭiyūḻiya uyarnilip paḷḷi (9,10,11 - vakuppu)
ti.pi. 1950-52(1919-21)	:	mukavi māvaṭṭam, tiruvilliputtūr vaṭṭam, cīyōṉ maliyiluḷḷa cīyōṉ naṭunilip paḷḷiyilmutaṟpaṭiva āciriyap paṇiyiṟ cērntār.
ti.pi. 1952-53(1921-22)	:	vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam, āmpūr naṭunilip paḷḷi yil (tām payiṉṟa paḷḷi) utavi āciriyarāyp paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1955 (1924)	:	matippu mikkatākak karutappaṭṭa maturit tamiḻc caṅkamnaṭattiya paṇṭitat tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār.(ï› vāṇṭil pāvāṇarit tavira vēṟoruvarumveṟṟi peṟavilli).
		utavittamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉi, tiruvallik kēṇik kellaṟṟu uyarnilip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1956 (1925)	:	tamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉik kiṟittavak kallūri uyarnilip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		ciṟuvar pāṭal tiraṭṭu nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1957 (1926)	:	tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiyat tamiḻc caṅkam naṭattiya taṉittamiḻp pulavar tērvil ivar oruvarē veṟṟi peṟṟār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
ti.pi. 1959 (1928)	:	talimit tamiḻāciriyar, maṉṉārkuṭi piṉlē kallūri uyarnilip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		mutal maṉivi ecuttar ammiyār maṟivu.
ti.pi. 1961 (1930)	:	nēcamaṇi ammiyāri maṇantār.
ti.pi. 1963 (1932)			kiṟittavak kīrttaṉam - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1965 (1934)	:	talimit tamiḻāciriyar, picappu īpar uyarnilip paḷḷi, puttūr,  tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟi ṉār.
		kīḻikkalit (B.O.L.) tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār.
ti.pi. 1968 (1937)	:	mutal inti etirppup pōri miyamākak koṇṭu - centamiḻkkāñci nūl veḷi yīṭu. inti etirppuk kiḻami koṇṭāṭiṉār.
		``kaṭṭurik kacaṭaṟi eṉṉum viyāca viḷakkam''nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1971 (1940)	:	oppiyaṉ moḻinūl mutaṟpākam
		iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇamākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
		kaṭṭuri variviyal eṉṉum urinaṭi          ilakkaṇa nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1972 (1941)	:	``tamiḻar carittirac curukkam''  veḷiyīṭu : tamiḻaka iḷiñar maṉṟam, tiruccirāppaḷḷi.
		``tamiḻaṉ eppaṭik keṭṭāṉ'' ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭal.
ti.pi. 1974 (1943)	:	talimit tamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉi muttiyālup pēṭṭi uyarnilip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1974 (1943)	:	cuṭṭu viḷakkam - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		- paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār talimiyil naṭi peṟṟa mutalām tamiḻ uṇarcci mānāṭṭil paṅkēṟṟu uriyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1975 (1944)	:	- tamiḻttuṟit talivarākac cēlam nakarāṇmik kallūriyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		- tiraviṭattāy - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1980 (1949)	:	collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷ - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		pāvāṇarkkut tavattiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār cāṉṟitaḻ vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittār.
		pāvāṇar avarkaḷ cēlam kallūriyil paṇi yāṟṟiyapōtu teṉmoḻi āciriyar peruñcittira ṉār avarkaḷ akkallūriyil payiṉṟār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
ti.pi. 1981 (1950)	:	uyartarak kaṭṭuri ilakkaṇam (mu.pā.) - nūl veḷiyīṭu.

ti.pi.1990 (1959)	:	tamiḻnāṭṭu araciṉ āṭcit tuṟiyil kalic col lākkattokuppil paṅkēṟṟuc ciṟappittamik kākat tamiḻnāṭṭaracu cārpil tamiḻnāṭṭu āḷunarāl  avarukkuc ceppup paṭṭayam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
		moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar avarkaḷāl peyar cūṭṭappaṭṭa `teṉmoḻi' itaḻ pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār avarkaḷāl toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. taṉittamiḻiyakka vaḷarccikku iv vitaḻ iṉṟaḷavum perum paṅkāṟṟi varukiṟatu.

		- aṇṇāmali palkalikkaḻakattil ēṟpaṭṭa cikkalāl eṉakku vaṟumiyum uṇṭu, maṉiviyum, makkaḷum uṇṭu - atōṭu eṉakku māṉamum uṇṭu - eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭup palkalikkaḻakap paṇiyiliruntu veḷiyēṟiṉār. eṉṉōṭu tamiḻum veḷiyēṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
